# DESIGN.md — Consuela (Home-ai)

<!-- impeccable:design-schema 1 -->

## Dashboard world (established, inherited)

- **Pastel-bento warm glass:** translucent frosted `widget-glass` surfaces on a soft warm gradient; 10-color accent system (`--color-accent-*`) with one selected accent (`--color-accent-selected`); high-contrast toggle supported.
- **Type:** Apple SF Pro stack utilities only. No paid or downloaded fonts.
- **Bento grid:** Home uses `auto-rows-[350px]`; widgets are 1×1 or 2×1 and must never spill their cell (contained scroll or `+N more` footer).
- **Visuals:** CSS/SVG/emoji only — no stock imagery, no icon libraries, no WebGL.
- **Motion:** CSS animations only (no framer-motion); state-driven, never decorative infinite loops; pause when tab hidden; every animation has a `prefers-reduced-motion` fallback.
- **Emoji as iconography:** liberal, lightweight, consistent with family-member emoji avatars.
- **Emergency + dialogs:** the floating emergency shield is locked to `--color-accent-rose` (rose means alarm only — never decoration); the shared `Modal` carries real dialog semantics (`role="dialog"`, `aria-modal`, `aria-labelledby`, focus trap, focus-return to the trigger) for every consumer.
- **Calendar day strip + glass-month title (2026-09-04):** the `/calendar` month-grid card carries a horizontal day strip (weekday letter over a numeral circle; selected = solid `--color-accent-button` gradient pill with white numeral, today = accent ring) sharing ONE `selectedDay` state with the grid; the month title replays a 240ms `calendarMonthSettle` rise per month change via key-remount. Both themes mirror; reduced-motion zeroes it.

## Weather card world — "(Not Boring), Consuela-style" (replacement world, 2026-08-27)

The weather card alone speaks (Not Boring) Weather's interface language, in Consuela's palette. Every other widget stays pastel-bento.

### Grammar
- Huge temperature as the hero object: SF Pro weight 800–900, tight tracking, `tabular-nums`.
- Data-true scene: cloud count/opacity track `cloud_cover`, particle velocity/drift track wind speed/direction, rain density tracks precipitation probability, fog tracks humidity, sun position tracks the real sun arc. No element decorates without data.
- One shared timeline: card day-strip preview and modal scrubber render through the same hour-state path.
- Minimal chrome; details live in dotted-leader rows (`HUMIDITY ··· 62%`) with tracked-uppercase labels and rounded metric pills.
- One accent per state: the skin's accent colors strip, rain ticks, and interactive elements only.

### Palettes
- **Day skins, auto-keyed to season (no user picker):**
  - Spring — pastel lemon + soft lilac
  - Summer — pastel guava (soft coral) + peach
  - Autumn — soft red pastel + warm amber
  - Winter — pastel ice blue + silver white
- **Night — full Not Boring:** near-black `#0A0A0A` base, white numerals, one condition accent (soft red / amber / cyan). Keyed to real `is_day`/sunset from the API, never a fixed clock time.
- Both palettes must hold AA contrast for all text at all times of day.

### Scene system
- Layered SVG/CSS: sky gradient (skin), sun/moon disc riding the sun arc, cloud forms, particle precipitation, fog layer, lightning flash for storm codes.
- Season changes the skin tint, not the illustration. Holiday overlays persist on top.
- Scene changes crossfade; values count up; hard cuts are banned.

### Interaction
- Card: press-and-drag the day strip to preview any hour (scene, temp, condition follow); release animates back to now. Tap elsewhere on the card → modal.
- Modal: 24h scrubber (`role="slider"` with spoken `aria-valuetext`), exploded metric rows, UV 5-dot scale, pressure, sun arc, hourly/daily toggle.
- Missing data hides rows; it never fakes values.

## Ask Consuela chat — "Consuela's kitchen table" (accent unification, 2026-09-04)

The chat surface joins the dashboard's accent system completely; its former fixed-violet identity is retired. Violet survives only as the default value of `--color-accent-selected` — pick another accent in Accent Studio and the whole chat follows.

### Grammar
- **The opening is the family's day, not a mascot screen.** Arrival order: who's speaking → dinner → next up → Consuela's open loops → composer. The orb is a ~72px companion beside the greeting; it swells (140px + ripple rings) only while thinking, then hands off to the thread.
- **Every tap is a draft.** Brief cards, open-loop chips, and suggestion chips fill the composer as editable text; nothing on this surface writes family data in one tap (kid-safety rule).
- **Open loops are real.** The chip row renders the suggestion engine's pending notices (kid-filtered via `visibleSuggestionsForRole`); static category drafts appear only as the empty-state fallback.
- **The thread tells its own story.** Telegram-mirrored messages wear a `via Telegram · {member}` origin label; the active thread keeps a one-line today strip (speaker · dinner · next) above the messages.
- **Zero raw palette literals.** All tints derive from `color-mix(in srgb, var(--color-accent-selected) N%, transparent)` — bubbles (18%/8%), chips (14–16%/5–6%), avatars (30%/15%), glows and ripples likewise. White-on-`--color-accent-button` for user bubbles only (the WCAG-safe white-text token).

### Honesty rules (carried from the polish pass)
- Signed-out chat says so (amber banner, thread-specific copy) — guest AI still answers, and silence would lie about the family thread.
- Empty states are real: "Nothing planned yet" dinner, "Quiet rest of day" events, engine-quiet fallback chips. Never invented data.
- **The orb can speak.** A 44px 🔊 companion beside the today-strip reads the last reply aloud for pre-readers (system voice, stripped of markdown/emoji); error replies are never read; it stops on tap. **The brief is alive** — it re-reads on the family data pulse and counts down to what's next. **Do-it is confirm-gated** — actionable loops carry a separate accent button through the shared PIN flow; the primary tap still drafts.

## Kid mode (wired 2026-09-04)

The kid experience is a mode, not a parallel app: `KidHome` renders behind `mode === "kid"` (any signed-in non-parent) on the same Home route, with kid-specific styling scoped in `src/modes/modes.css` (kid-scoped selectors only — no global overrides). The capsule nav gains a 🎁 Rewards entry (House is swapped out; both modes stay 7 items so the ≥44px-at-390px sizing holds). Quest completion runs through the shared PIN flow (`verifyPinRemote` — server-side verification, PINs never client-side), rewards redeem through `RewardsShop` with the same >100pts parent-approval gate as the Tasks page, and the celebration fires only after a real, confirmed success.

## Calendar truth + honest failures (wired 2026-09-04)

- **Google events wear their calendar's color.** Every synced event carries its owning calendar's Google `colorRgb` (`colorHex` from `src/lib/calendar/google-mapping.ts`) on rows and month dots — cyan is the fallback, never a guess. Color is data, not decoration.
- **A PIN failure says which kind it is.** The shared seam `verifyPinRemote` (`src/modes/kid/kid-store.ts`) returns `ok | wrongPin | unreachable`; every gate (kid quest, redeem, parent approval, all seven Tasks gates) distinguishes "Couldn't reach Consuela" from "Wrong PIN" and clears the input on both paths. Offline is never blamed on the user.
- **The calendar-member roster is live-primed.** `src/lib/calendar-member-snapshot.ts` primes its cached snapshot at subscribe time, so events that fired while a surface was unmounted are recovered, not lost. Contract for any future surface using it: subscribe → prime → re-read on dispatch, and pass the CLIENT snapshot as `useSyncExternalStore` arg 2 (the deterministic server fallback is arg 3 — swapping them silently pins the UI to placeholders forever).

# UI Navigation, Layout & UI Change Records

> Moved verbatim from AGENTS.md §1 (1.1–1.4 + all `### UI Change Record` blocks) on 2026-09-24. The UI Change Records below are the **binding per-feature contracts** ("CONTRACTS to keep") — when a shipped change alters UI behavior, its record goes HERE, never into AGENTS.md.

## 1. User Interface (UI) Navigation

### 1.1 Current Layout & Navigation Model

The dashboard is a mobile-first, glass-morphism, bottom-nav app with a persistent floating action for emergencies.

**Capsule Nav (always present, `CapsuleNav.tsx`):** a floating bottom-center glass capsule. The active item expands into a neon-lime pill (green icon circle + label); inactive items are compact dark circles. Width expansion is a CSS-only `0fr→1fr` grid-column transition; the whole capsule auto-scales down on narrow viewports (`--capsule-scale`). Seven items for adults: Home, Ask, Meals, Tasks, Calendar, House (`/ha`, Home Assistant controls), Settings — the House item is hidden for the child role (kid mode keeps the original 6 tabs). Items are `<button>`s with `aria-label` + `aria-current="page"`.

| Label          | Route          | Icon (active = heavier stroke / filled)          | Primary Notes |
|----------------|----------------|--------------------------------------------------|---------------|
| Home           | `/`            | House                                            | Dashboard with weather, today's events, quick AI ask, weekly meals preview, tasks |
| Ask            | `/chat`        | Speech bubble with 3 dots (primary, filled when active) | Main conversational interface |
| Meals          | `/meals`       | Pot / plate                                      | Kitchen loop: 🍽️ Plan (weekly meal grid + inline Recipe box) → 🛒 Shop (store-aware grocery list with price comparison, Instacart ordering per store or all stores at once, Ask Instacart buttons + Clem AI grocery assistant 🛒 FAB, ShopGuide how-to card) → 🥫 Stock (pantry + low/out → grocery sync) |
| Tasks          | `/tasks`       | Checklist                                        | Chore list with points |
| Calendar       | `/calendar`    | Calendar grid (rect + binding rings + day dots) | Family routines, events, and month view |
| House          | `/ha`        | Sliders (3 vertical faders)               | Home Assistant controls: Overview, Security (arm/disarm), Climate, Lights, Automations. Hidden for kids (child role) |
| Settings       | `/settings`    | Gear                                             | Far-right tab. Theme (dark/light/system + 10 accents + high-contrast), family members, emergency contacts config |

**Floating Emergency Button (always on Home, `EmergencyButton.tsx`):**
- Fixed position: `top-4 right-4`, `z-50`
- Rose TOKEN circle (`--color-accent-rose`, ≥44px) with white shield + exclamation SVG
- Taps → modal with 4 serious types: 🔥 Fire, 💧 Water Leak, 🤕 Injury, 🚨 General
- On selection → POST `/api/emergency` with PIN from auth context → dual notification (SMS via carrier email-to-SMS + HTML email via Gmail App Password)
- **PIN required:** The endpoint verifies the PIN against stored family member PINs. Unauthenticated requests return 401.
- Success state: persistent success screen — green check + "Alert Sent" + "Sent to X of Y contacts" + "If this is a life-threatening emergency, call 911." + explicit Done button (no auto-close); PIN cleared on close
- Failure: red X + "Try Again" + advice to call 911 directly

**Recent Visual Language (as of adbe770):**
- Glass / translucent surfaces with 20px blur
- Isometric cards with perspective hover lift (`isometric-card` class)
- Gradient orbs as decorative background elements (pulse + melt animations)
- Safe-area insets respected for iOS notch / home indicator

**How to document future UI updates (mandatory for the agent):**
Use this exact delta format in the "What's New" area and update 1.5 journeys:

```markdown
### UI Change Record — 2026-06-12 — Liquid Glass cards for Today / Daily Schedule / Tasks
- Added / Changed: `src/app/page.tsx` (Today + Tasks items), `src/components/ui/ScheduleDisplay.tsx` (Schedule items + empty state)
- Visual / Motion: New `.liquid-glass` class in `src/app/globals.css`. Each row is now a 3D "bubble": color-tinted linear-gradient frosting (rgba 0.32 → 0.16 across 135°), 20px backdrop blur, 1.25rem squircle radius, bright `inset 0 1px 0` top highlight + soft `inset 0 -1px 0` bottom edge, 0/8/24 drop shadow that deepens to 0/14/32 on hover with a 3px lift. Light-mode variant swaps the inner highlight to a brighter 0.85 white edge and softens the drop shadow. Accent bar shrunk from w-1 to w-0.5 and given an 8px currentColor glow. Member / points pills moved from solid `surface-3` to `glass-subtle`. Reduced-motion disables the lift.
- Color frosting sources: Today events use the event color (mint/violet/amber/cyan/rose/nori). Tasks use priority (rose > 15pts, amber > 10pts, mint default). Daily Schedule uses the schedule item color.
- Agent action required: Update this section + Common Journeys + any affected SOP
- User-facing description (copy-paste ready for responses):
  > "On the Home screen the Today events, Daily Schedule, and Tasks cards now feel like little 3D bubbles — each row has a frosted glass surface with a light frosting of its existing color, a bright top highlight, and a soft drop shadow that lifts the card off the background. Hover to see them gently rise."
- Added / Changed: <component or page>
- Visual / Motion: <describe exactly what user sees, file paths, CSS classes>
- Agent action required: Update this section + Common Journeys + any affected SOP
- User-facing description (copy-paste ready for responses):
  > "On the Home screen the chat bubble now gently floats up and down..."
```

### UI Change Record — 2026-09-23 — Server-authoritative task approvals
- Added / Changed: NEW `src/app/api/tasks/approve/route.ts` (approve/approve-all/send-back actions — parent PIN via `verifyPinFromPB`, snapshot-primary task lookup, entire pay under `withWeekLedgerLock` → snapshot keyed lock, pays `pendingApproval.points`, per-payee `taskId+member` reversal-aware idempotency, crew send-back strips `checkedInAt`, approve-all takes explicit `taskIds` and fails closed on unknown ids), `src/lib/snapshot-tasks.ts` (shared `persistSnapshotWeek` — claim route now imports it), `src/lib/task-utils.ts` (B1 fix — amount = `pendingApproval.points ?? task.points`), `src/middleware.ts` (exempts `/api/tasks/approve`), `src/app/tasks/page.tsx` (client `submitApproval` POSTs and adopts server `weekData`; offline keeps local approval), tests: NEW `tests/unit/task-approve-route` + extended pending-flow, approve-all, middleware-exempt, pending-approval (B1 amount).
- Visual / Motion: No visual redesign (correctness only — speed-bonus points now appear, double-pay impossible, crew send-back reopens). `prefers-reduced-motion` untouched.
- Color sources: None — no palette changes.
- **CONTRACTS to keep:**
  - `/api/tasks/approve` is middleware-exempt and self-gates on parent PIN — never add a session/PIN-free approval path; child/pet PIN → 403 `adult_only`.
  - Amount = `pendingApproval.points ?? task.points` — never pay `task.points` alone (B1).
  - Idempotency = `taskId + member`, reversal-aware — never regress to `taskId`-only (B6/crew).
  - Lock order week-ledger → snapshot-keyed only; `persistSnapshotWeek` is the shared seam.
  - `approve-all` sends explicit `taskIds` and fails closed on unknown ids.
  - Client stays optimistic + adopts server `weekData`; offline keeps local approval (D8).
  - Send-back on crew strips `checkedInAt`, preserves `joinedAt`/`removed` (B4).
  - Verified: typecheck clean, eslint 0 on touched files, full suite green (2 pre-existing teardown exceptions), `next build` clean.

### UI Change Record — 2026-09-20 — Ask Consuela: thread visible while thinking + streams survive tab navigation
- Added / Changed: NEW `src/lib/chat-store.ts` (module-level chat singleton — state `{ messages, isTyping, statusLine, hydrated, streaming }`, actions `ensureHydrated()` / `send(text, speaker)` / `stop()` / `startNewConversation()` / `retry(text, id, speaker)`, the `useSyncExternalStore` pair `subscribe`/`getSnapshot`/`getServerSnapshot`, and the `__resetChatStoreForTests()` seam); `src/app/chat/page.tsx` (messages/stream state removed — the page now subscribes to the store and keeps only UI-local state; `showHero = !hasRealMessages`; the hero's 200px thinking-orb branch deleted; composer wired to the store's `streaming` flag; localStorage persistence moved into the store); tests: NEW `tests/unit/chat-store.test.ts` (7) + 2 page regressions in `chat-page-stream.test.tsx`; `chat-conversation-reset` / `chat-points-chip` / `chat-speaker-roster-refresh` suites gain the reset seam.
- Visual / Motion: The first message now lands in the normal thread immediately — your bubble with the ✨ typing bubble (and any tool status line) beneath it — instead of the screen swapping to the full-width orb. Send/stop behavior is unchanged (the composer locks for the whole stream, editable while waiting). No new keyframes; the idle hero orb, ripples, and reduced-motion contract are untouched.
- Color sources: None — logic + layout only, existing warm-glass tokens.
- Agent action required: Update this section + "Current Dashboard Snapshot" + Change Log. **CONTRACTS to keep:** (1) `src/lib/chat-store.ts` is the single owner of chat messages AND the live stream — never reintroduce a page-local `messages` useState or a second stream driver. (2) `showHero` must stay `!hasRealMessages` — never gate the thread on `isTyping` again (that was the bug). (3) `getServerSnapshot()` must return the frozen seed state (`[greeting]`, `hydrated:false`) so SSR/first paint stays byte-identical. (4) `ensureHydrated()` is once-per-page-load; remounts do an incremental `since` reconcile and are skipped while `streamInFlight`. (5) hydration merges (never replaces) via `mergeThread`, and `Message.at` stays the only sort key. (6) every chat page suite must call `__resetChatStoreForTests()` in `beforeEach` (module singleton). (7) zero changes to `/api/hermes/chat` and `/api/chat/messages`. Verified: chat suites 58/58, tsc clean, eslint 0 on touched files, full suite 2362 passed (323 files; 2 pre-existing uncaught teardown exceptions in `tasks-approve-all`, reproduce in isolation), `next build` clean.
- User-facing description (copy-paste ready for responses):
  > "Ask Consuela keeps your conversation on screen now. When you send a message, your bubble and the little thinking dots stay right where they are — the screen no longer wipes the conversation and shows a big spinning orb while she works. And if you switch to another tab while she's answering, she keeps going: come back and her reply is there, already written or still arriving. Nothing is lost and nothing needs a page change to appear."

  > "Tasks now come in three kinds. **Assigned** is the classic one-owner chore. **Open** is up for grabs — nobody owns it, and the first person to claim it earns a speed bonus. **Crew** needs several helpers: everyone joins, taps "Done my part," and when the whole crew checks in a parent approves once and **every crew member earns the full points**. There's an Open board at the top of the Tasks page, and kids can join crews right on their home screen. Parents can also remove someone who never checked in so the rest aren't stuck. Recurring crew chores come back each week with a fresh, empty crew."

### UI Change Record — 2026-09-20 — Tasks-screen lift: safety gates, hierarchy, kid language, session controls, parent efficiency
- Added / Changed: `src/app/tasks/page.tsx` (P0 gates + delete-confirm Modal + kid redirect + P1 reorder + P3 Approve-all/Stepper/select), `src/modes/kid/quest-labels.ts` (NEW pure kidDueLabel/dueTone/questWhoLabel), `src/modes/kid/QuestCard.tsx` (due chips + who-line), `src/modes/kid/KidHome.tsx` (quests first, Switch member everywhere, countdown chip), `src/modes/kid/RewardsShop.tsx` (mode-end redirect), `src/hooks/useAuth.tsx` (role-aware 15-min kid timeout + 5-min warn), `src/components/ui/SegmentedControl.tsx` (min-h-44), `src/components/ui/Stepper.tsx` (aria-labeled +/-), `src/components/ui/AnimatedEmoji.tsx` (11 keyframes hoisted), `src/app/globals.css` (hoisted keyframes + .member-tile-name .7rem), DELETED `src/components/tasks/RemindersSection.tsx` + `src/hooks/useGoogleReminders.ts` (dead integration).

The Leaderboard tab opens on the podium + prize race. Kids see "Today/⚠️ Late" chips and "🫳 Up for grabs" instead of ISO dates and strangers' names; a one-tap "🔄 Switch member" sits under their hero. No new keyframes; every existing motion contract (Modal spring/settle, confetti, reduced-motion kills) untouched.
- Color sources: Existing tokens only — mint/cyan/rose due chips, amber countdown, text-secondary muted. No new palette entries.
- Agent action required: Update this section + "Current Dashboard Snapshot". **CONTRACTS to keep:** (1) Add/Edit/Delete on /tasks is PARENT-ONLY and Delete always confirms — never re-expose edit affordances to kids/guests. (2) role child|pet on /tasks redirects to "/" — kid flows live on KidHome only. (3) kidDueLabel/dueTone/questWhoLabel are the ONLY quest due/who rendering — never print a raw ISO date or a foreign assignee name on a kid quest. (4) kid sessions are 15 min (KID_INACTIVITY_TIMEOUT_MS) with a 5-min warn; adults 30/30s — role read at tick time from currentUserRef. (5) "Approve all" loops the same idempotent approvePendingCompletion — never a bulk shortcut that bypasses the per-row guards. (6) Stepper derives Decrease/Increase labels from its label prop. Verified: live NAS probes (guest 390px: Pending@562px, 0 tiny, 0 sub-44, 0 style tags; kid /tasks→"/" kid mode; quest chips + Switch member live), full suite 2348 passed / 3 pre-existing, tsc clean, eslint 0, next build clean.
- User-facing description (copy-paste ready for responses):
  > "The Tasks screen got a full lift. For parents: your chore list is now the first thing on the page, the 'Consuela suggests' card waits quietly at the bottom until it actually has ideas, the leaderboard tab opens on the podium and prize race, and you can approve every tapped chore with one PIN instead of one per row. For kids: quests say 'Today' or '⚠️ Late' instead of dates, open chores say 'Up for grabs' instead of showing a sibling's name, there's a big one-tap 'Switch member' button, and the screen hands itself back to the family after 15 quiet minutes (with a 5-minute warning first). And chores are safe now — kids can't add, edit, or delete family tasks anymore, and deleting always asks first."

### UI Change Record — 2026-09-18 — Shop grocery rows: mobile `⋯` overflow sheet — product names visible on phones
- Added / Changed: NEW `src/components/meals/GroceryItemActionsSheet.tsx` (shared-Modal consumer — portaled to `document.body`, always-mounted, titled `{emoji} {name}`, full-width token-styled action rows: 🥫 Send to pantry (checked rows only, `pantryBusy`-gated), 📌 Lock/Unlock auto-sync (label flips on `manualOverride`), ✎ Edit details, 🗑️ Delete (rose)), `src/components/meals/ShopTab.tsx` (trailing restructure: StorePill stays visible at ALL sizes; Pantry button + priority Chip + lock + edit + delete moved UNCHANGED inside a `hidden sm:flex items-center gap-1.5` wrapper; new `sm:hidden` ⋯ button — `aria-label="More actions for {name}"`, 3-dot SVG, `stopPropagation`, `h-9 w-9` + `.hit-44` = 36px visual / 44px effective; split-state wiring: `actionsItemId` (matching id) + `actionsSheetItem` (item copy), `open={actionsItemId !== null}`, `item={groceryItems.find(…) ?? actionsSheetItem}` — live-resolves while open, the stale copy carries the 150ms Modal exit so the close animation plays, `onClose` nulls only the id; leading checkbox OUTER button shrunk `h-11 w-11` → `h-9 w-9` + `.hit-44`, inner `h-7 w-7` visual square untouched), NEW `scripts/consuela/verify-shop-mobile-actions.mjs` (34-check Playwright probe — guest state, 390×844 + 1280×800, dark + light: title `clientWidth ≥ 140` (floor amended 150→140 — the original arithmetic omitted SectionCard body padding; measured ~10px before → 125-132px after the collapse → 141/148/148px after hit-44), full name in textContent, no horizontal overflow, ⋯ visible/cluster display-hidden @390, sheet flows (open/Delete/lock→unlock flip/StorePill→StorePicker), desktop cluster visible + ⋯ hidden @1280, 0 page errors), tests: NEW `tests/unit/grocery-item-actions-sheet.test.tsx` (5) + `tests/unit/shop-tab-mobile-actions.test.tsx` (5); existing `grocery-handoff`/`grocery-override-ui` suites unchanged and green (desktop cluster stays in the DOM; jsdom ignores Tailwind gates). Commits 8324694, 9eadb5a, 089be56, 6bb1db6, 1293c65.
- Visual / Motion: On phones (<640px) a grocery row is now: checkbox · emoji + full name · store pill · ⋯. The trailing cluster (Pantry button, priority Chip, lock, edit, delete) is display:none below sm and the title takes its width back — names read in full instead of truncating to nothing (~10px). Tapping ⋯ raises the shared bottom sheet (spring enter, settle exit, portaled above the capsule nav) titled with the item, offering Send to pantry (checked rows only), Lock/Unlock auto-sync, Edit details, and a rose Delete. On ≥sm screens rows are byte-identical to before (cluster visible, ⋯ hidden). No new keyframes; `prefers-reduced-motion` untouched (sheet motion is the existing Modal contract).
- Color sources: Existing tokens only — `--color-surface-2`/`--color-surface-3` sheet rows, `--color-accent-rose` Delete (rose text + rose hover wash), `--color-accent-mint` Pantry action, `--color-accent-amber` locked state. No new palette entries.
- Agent action required: Update this section + "Current Dashboard Snapshot" + Change Log. **CONTRACTS to keep:** (1) the sheet is a Modal consumer → it portals to `document.body`, so tests must assert against `document.body`, never the mount root — and it mounts unconditionally: the split state (`actionsItemId` + `actionsSheetItem`) keeps `item` alive through the 150ms Modal exit (exit-animation contract) — never gate the mount on `open`; (2) `hidden sm:flex` / `sm:hidden` are the ONLY responsive gates — jsdom ignores them, so unit tests must scope sheet queries outside the mount root (the desktop cluster stays in the DOM at every width); (3) the row-cluster and sheet action aria-labels are intentionally identical (`lock {name} from auto-sync` / `unlock {name} for auto-sync` / `Edit {name}` / `Delete {name}`) — never both live in real browsers (each is display:none at the other's breakpoint) — keep them in sync when renaming; (4) ≥640px rows must stay byte-identical — future row actions go in BOTH the inline cluster and the sheet; (5) `.hit-44` is the sanctioned seam for the checkbox wrapper + ⋯ (36px visual / 44px effective) — prefer it over re-varnishing sizes; (6) the probe's 140px title floor is measured-tight (worst case 141 clears it by 1px — fragile if row content changes) — re-run `node scripts/consuela/verify-shop-mobile-actions.mjs` when touching row content; (7) the priority Chip is phones-hidden BY USER DECISION and is NOT in the sheet (still on ≥sm rows, settable via Edit details) — don't add it to the sheet. Verified: probe **34/34 ALL CHECKS PASSED**, new suites 10/10, `grocery-handoff`/`grocery-override-ui` green, tsc clean, eslint 0 on touched files, full suite 2239 passed / 2 failed (308 files — only the 2 documented pre-existing TZ-streak failures; the initially-reported full-suite-only `shop-tab-mobile-actions` failures were a harness race — the sheet's 150ms Modal closing timer firing into a `document.body` wiped by the next test — resolved by unmounting the test root in `afterEach`, which clears the timer before the wipe).
- User-facing description (copy-paste ready for responses):
  > "The grocery list is readable on your phone again. Each row now shows the item's full name instead of squeezing it to nothing next to a row of little buttons — on small screens the Pantry, priority, lock, edit, and delete buttons are gathered behind a small ⋯ button at the end of the row. Tap ⋯ and everything is there in a pop-up sheet: send a checked item to the pantry, lock the item so meal-plan sync leaves it alone, edit its details, or delete it. The store pill stays right on the row, and on tablets and computers everything looks exactly the same as before."

### UI Change Record — 2026-09-18 — Ask Consuela: deterministic thread ordering + a genuinely fresh new conversation
- Added / Changed: NEW `src/lib/chat-thread.ts` (pure — `threadKey`, `sortThread`, `mergeThread`, `lastResetIndex`, `visibleThread`, `SEED_GREETING_ID`), `src/app/chat/page.tsx` (`Message` gains an `at` epoch-ms sort key set on every row — PB rows derive it from `createdAt`, optimistic rows use `Date.now()`, legacy/seeds default to 0; both hydration effects now `mergeThread(prev, …)` instead of `setMessages(replace)`; NEW `visibleMessages` memo drives the message map + `userMessageCount`/`showHero`/`showQuickActions`/`lastAssistantReply`; `showHero` now hands the view to the thread once any real message exists, not only when a user row exists; the seed greeting is never persisted and is dropped from the view once the user has spoken; the "New conversation" confirm copy now says the screen clears while PB keeps history), `src/db/pb-db.ts` (`selectChatMessages` sort `"createdAt,id"` — deterministic tiebreak), tests: NEW `tests/unit/chat-thread.test.ts` (18) + a delayed-hydration ordering test in `chat-page-stream.test.tsx` (and its stale "resets to the greeting" comment corrected).
- Visual / Motion: Behavioural, not a restyle. A message sent before the thread finishes loading now stays put with its reply below it; a backdated Telegram row lands in its chronological slot instead of the bottom. Starting a new conversation clears the screen to the hero/fresh state (the divider frames anything sent afterward), immediately, after a reload, and on other devices — older messages remain in PB but are no longer rendered. No new keyframes; `prefers-reduced-motion` untouched.
- Color sources: None — no palette changes.
- Agent action required: Update this section + "Current Dashboard Snapshot" + the "How do I start a new conversation with Consuela?" journey + Change Log. **CONTRACTS to keep:** (1) `Message.at` is the ONLY sort key — never reintroduce positional append/replace (`mergePBThread` is deleted; use `mergeThread`); (2) `mergeThread` is strictly additive — hydration must never remove an optimistic row; (3) the reset `system` marker is both the visible divider and the LLM-context cutoff — `visibleThread` and the `messagesRef` history slice both key on the newest marker; (4) `threadKey` deliberately omits `speaker` (accepted edge: two different members posting identical text inside one reconcile window may collapse to one row); (5) the seed greeting is identified by id+content — never persist it. **This supersedes the 2026-09-09 steering record's "the whole day stays readable above the divider" claim** — the pre-marker day is now hidden from the view but still kept on the server.
- User-facing description (copy-paste ready for responses):
  > "Ask Consuela got two fixes. First, if you typed fast enough that the chat was still loading your history, your message could end up below Consuela's reply — messages are now ordered by when they happened, so your question always sits above its answer. Second, starting a new conversation now actually clears the screen (to a fresh '✨ New conversation' start) instead of leaving the old messages on display — the old messages are still safely stored on the family server, they're just not shown. And signed-out chats no longer show a duplicate line under their own message."

No new motion; `prefers-reduced-motion` untouched.

- Agent action required: Update this section + "Current Dashboard Snapshot" + Change Log. **CONTRACTS to keep:** (1) ALL Google day-membership goes through `googleEventCoversDay`/`googleEventCoveredDays` ONLY — never re-add a start_iso prefix/`~`-contains day filter to a new surface; (2) the Google fetch is best-effort — a failure must leave family events intact (guest/session-expired devices stay honest); (3) `db.selectTodaysEvents()` remains the family-`events`-only read — the merge happens at the page level, consistent with the Calendar page owning its own merge.
- User-facing description (copy-paste ready for responses):
  > "The calendar card on the Home screen now shows your Google Calendar events too, not just events added inside the dashboard. Multi-day events like 'Bailey & Emily at home!' appear on every day they cover, all-day events read 'All day,' and each event wears its own calendar's color — the same way the Calendar page already showed them."

### UI Change Record — 2026-09-14 — Tiered house-alert notifications (weather + calendar push, routines in-app, "What to send" toggles)
- Added / Changed: NEW `src/lib/weather-severity.ts` (pure WMO storm `code≥95` + heavy-snow `{73,75,85,86}` sets + `severeFamily()` — single source of truth, re-exported from `WeatherSkins.ts` so `WeatherWidget` is untouched), NEW `src/lib/ha/alerts.ts` (pure cores: `withinQuietHours`, `weatherEpisodeDecision`, `calendarLeadDecisions`, `IMPORTANCE_THRESHOLD/CALENDAR_LEAD_MS/ROUTINE_LEAD_MS/QUIET_*`), NEW `src/lib/ha/weather-alert-fetch.ts` (`readSevereWeather` — Open-Meteo current+hourly reduced to `{code, severeEndISO}`), NEW crons `src/app/api/cron/consuela/{weather-alert,calendar-alert}/route.ts` (bearer `isCronAuthorized`, pref-gated, `broadcastHouseAlert` fan-out, mark alerted only when `sent>0`, degrade to `200 {ok:false}`), NEW `/api/ha/notify-prefs` route (GET defaults / POST `{key,enabled}` create-on-404 upsert), `src/lib/pb-seed.ts` (NEW `ha_alert_state` collection; `seedNotifyPrefs` seeds `briefing|weather|calendar` rows OFF idempotently; `proactive_suggestions.kind` select gains `routine_due`), `src/lib/consuela/engine.ts` (NEW `scanRoutinesDue` registered in `runEngine`; `fetchExistingConditionKeys` now filters `expiresAt=""||>now`), `src/components/settings/HaNotificationsCard.tsx` (new **"What to send"** 3-toggle block reading/writing notify-prefs). Tests: NEW `weather-severity`, `ha-alerts`, `ha-notify-prefs-route`, `ha-weather-alert-route`, `ha-calendar-alert-route`, `engine-routine-due`, `pb-seed-notify-prefs` + a card prefs case in `ha-settings-ui.test.tsx`. Spec/plan: `docs/superpowers/specs|plans/2026-09-14-tiered-house-alert-notifications*` (outer repo).
- Visual / Motion: Settings → Integrations → 🔔 Notifications card grows a "What to send" section under the phone/Telegram target toggles — three glass toggle rows (Morning briefing / Severe weather / Important calendar events, each with a one-line hint) that POST `/api/ha/notify-prefs` on flip with optimistic state. Nothing animates; reuses the existing `Toggle` + card-row material. New alerts themselves are push notifications to HA companion phones + the family Telegram chat (same channel as Emergency/briefing), never an on-screen element.
- Color sources: None new — existing amber card token + surface/text tokens only.
- Agent action required: Update this section + "Current Dashboard Snapshot" + §5.2 cron table + Change Log. **CONTRACTS to keep:** (1) severity codes live ONLY in `src/lib/weather-severity.ts` — import, never re-declare (both `WeatherSkins` and the server do); (2) a push is marked alerted in `ha_alert_state` ONLY when `broadcastHouseAlert().sent > 0`, else it retries next run; (3) prefs `key ∈ {briefing,weather,calendar}` default OFF and are opt-in — never flip a live family's choice on reseed (`seedNotifyPrefs` is create-if-absent); (4) quiet hours are 21:00–07:00 evaluated in the pure cores against family TZ, not at the route; (5) `fetchExistingConditionKeys` must ignore expired pending rows so `routine_due` re-fires daily (its `expiresAt` is the routine start). Verified: new suites green (weather-severity, ha-alerts 8, notify-prefs 6, weather-alert 5, calendar-alert 5, routine-due 2, pb-seed-prefs 2, card toggle), tsc clean on touched files.
- User-facing description (copy-paste ready for responses):
  > "Consuela can now tap you on the shoulder. In Settings → Notifications you'll find a new 'What to send' section with three switches — Morning briefing, Severe weather, and Important calendar events (all off until you turn them on). Turn on Severe weather and you'll get one heads-up when a real storm or big snow hits (quiet overnight — it waits for morning). Turn on Important calendar events and you'll get a nudge about an hour before a doctor, dentist, game, or anything flagged important. And 'Consuela suggests' now surfaces your family routines as they come up, so you'll see 'School pickup · at 8:20 AM' right in the feed."

### UI Change Record — 2026-09-15 — Weekly Prize Race (parent-set podium prizes, race-gap lines, all-time labeling, Monday win ceremony)
- Added / Changed: `src/lib/pb-seed.ts` (NEW `weekly_prizes` collection — `rank` required number + `emoji`/`text`, UNIQUE `idx_weekly_prizes_rank`; `hall_of_fame` gains optional `prize` text + `celebrated` bool; NEW `seedWeeklyPrizes` creates the 3 default rows if absent, never overwrites a parent's edits), `src/lib/db-gateway.ts` (`weekly_prizes` + `hall_of_fame` in the allowlist, parent write policy), `src/lib/task-utils.ts` (NEW: `WEEKLY_PRIZES_KEY` `consuela-weekly-prizes` + LWW stamp key, `DEFAULT_WEEKLY_PRIZES` (🥇 "Picks Friday's family movie" / 🥈 "Chooses the dessert night" / 🥉 "+$2 allowance"), `loadWeeklyPrizes`/`saveWeeklyPrizes`/`prizeForRank`/`touchWeeklyPrizesStamp`/`readWeeklyPrizesStamp`/`writeWeeklyPrizesStamp`, pure `raceGap` (competition rank + gap-to-last-prize-rank), `uncelebratedWinFor`/`markWinCelebrated`; `archiveWeekWinner` now records the TOP 3 with prize text frozen per rank — idempotent per member+weekStart, ZERO-point weeks record nothing, hall trim re-keyed from 12 entries to the latest 12 WEEKS), `src/db/index.ts` + `pb-db.ts` (`upsertWeeklyPrize` + `updateHallOfFameEntry` passthroughs), `src/components/settings/WeeklyPrizesCard.tsx` (NEW parent-only 🏆 card — 3 rank rows (medal + emoji + text), "Add prize" hidden at 3, removal re-packs ranks contiguously, save = local write + LWW stamp + one rank-keyed upsert per row + 🏆 toast; footer "Winners are locked in when the week resets Monday."), `src/components/leaderboard/PrizeRaceCard.tsx` (NEW Tasks Leaderboard card — one glass row per prize with the current holder(s) recomputed by competition rank from points (entry.rank is never trusted; ties share the rank, so a rank can hold two names and the next rank can sit "Up for grabs"), "⏳ Resets in N day(s)" countdown via `getDaysUntilWeekReset`, and a signed-in-only `aria-live` personal line: 🏆 lead / 👏 in a prize spot / "You're {N} pts from a prize — keep going!" targeting the LOWEST configured prize rank (two prizes → gap chases #2) / "Earn points to join this week's race!" for zero-point members; whole card + personal line hidden when no prizes are configured or the week has no points), `src/components/leaderboard/Podium.tsx` (slot gains a 🎁 prize ribbon from `prizeForRank(prizes, entry.rank)` — rendered only when the slot has points > 0, `title={prize.text}` survives truncation — plus a `10px "{N} all-time"` line shown only when all-time ≠ the week), `src/components/leaderboard/YourCard.tsx` ("· {N} all-time" at text-xs after the weekly points), `src/app/tasks/page.tsx` (Earned StatTile → "Earned this week" + "{N} pts all-time" detail following the member filter; mounts `PrizeRaceCard` + `WeeklyWinModal`; the Weekly Champ 🥇 badge now earns from any rank-1 hall entry), `src/modes/kid/KidHome.tsx` (hero gains the all-time caption keyed by the ledger-resolved full name via `resolveMemberName`; the kid leaderboard gains a positive-only race line — "🎉 You're winning {prize}!" / "{N} more points to win {prize}!" / "Earn points to win this week's prize!" — never loss-framed), `src/components/leaderboard/WeeklyWinModal.tsx` (NEW — mounts on Home (wall profile excluded), Tasks, KidHome; the newest prized rank≤3 hall entry with `celebrated !== true` for the signed-in member opens a confetti + "You won: {prize}" modal; BOTH buttons + scrim/Esc claim it: local `markWinCelebrated` first, then best-effort `POST /api/hall-of-fame/celebrate {memberName, weekStart}` — guests see nothing), NEW `src/app/api/hall-of-fame/celebrate/route.ts` (session-gated; child/pet sessions may claim ONLY their OWN win, parents any member — the gateway's parent-only hall write policy made kid self-claims impossible without it; unknown/zero-prize week → 404; already-celebrated → idempotent 200 no-op; sets `celebrated: true` server-side so the ceremony is single-fire across devices), `src/components/leaderboard/HomeLeaderboardWidget.tsx` (`PRIZE_TEXT_MAX = 30` clip + `PrizeRaceLine` bottom-slot line) + `src/components/leaderboard/hooks/useWeeklyPrizes.ts` (NEW shared hook — mounted-gated, re-reads on `consuela-data-refreshed`). Tests: NEW `weekly-prizes` (11), `weekly-prizes-card` (7), `prize-race-card` (10), `leaderboard-all-time-labels` (14), `pb-seed-weekly-prizes` (6), win-ceremony/celebrate-route/champ-badge/home+kid race-line suites across tasks 1–12. Spec/plan `docs/superpowers/specs|plans/2026-09-15-weekly-prize-race*` (outer repo).
- Visual / Motion: The Settings card rides the standard Rewards-neighborhood SectionCard language (🏆 tone `#f59e0b`). On the leaderboard the race card reads as three quiet glass rows — medal, prize, who's holding it right now (or "Up for grabs") — with the countdown up top and a single personal line at the bottom; podium winners get a small 🎁 ribbon under their points; the champion card, podium, and rows are otherwise unchanged. The Monday ceremony is the existing shared Modal (spring enter, settle exit, portaled to body) plus the EXISTING confetti burst — no new keyframes anywhere in the feature (the win modal's matchMedia guard and the page's existing `prefers-reduced-motion` kill blocks cover every motion). All-time lines are deliberately quiet (10–12px `text-text-muted`) so weekly numbers stay the big read.
- Color sources: Existing tokens only — race card + Settings card tone `#f59e0b` (the leaderboard amber family), holder names `text-[var(--color-accent-amber)]`, personal line `text-text-secondary`, ribbons/all-time lines `text-text-muted`. No new palette entries.
- Agent action required: Update this section + "Current Dashboard Snapshot" + Common User Journeys + Change Log. **CONTRACTS to keep:** (1) `weekly_prizes` upserts are rank-keyed with the UNIQUE `idx_weekly_prizes_rank` index (run `npm run pb:seed` on deploy — self-heals); the Settings card is the ONLY editor and stays parent-rendered (kids/guests never see it). (2) Prize edits ride the LWW stamp (`touchWeeklyPrizesStamp` BEFORE the push) and re-pack ranks contiguous 1..N — never leave a hole. (3) `hall_of_fame.prize` is written ONCE at rollover and never updated afterward (frozen history); zero-point weeks record NO entries; the hall keeps the latest 12 WEEKS not 12 rows. (4) The win ceremony must stay single-fire: local flag immediately + the celebrate route flips the server row; never re-show on dismiss-without-claim (every close path IS a claim). (5) Kid-facing race copy stays positive-only (no "you're losing"); the wall profile never mounts `WeeklyWinModal`. (6) competition rank is recomputed from points on every race surface — never trust a stored `entry.rank` for holders. Verified: full suite **2095/2095 (290 files)**, tsc clean, eslint 0 on touched files, `next build` clean, `npm run pb:seed` idempotent on the live instance.
- User-facing description (copy-paste ready for responses):
  > "Tasks now come with weekly prizes. Grown-ups set them in Settings (like '🥇 picks Friday's movie'), and all week the leaderboard shows who's holding each prize, how long until Monday's reset, and for whoever's signed in, how far away their prize is. When the week resets, the top three are locked into the Hall of Fame and each winner gets a little celebration with their prize the next time they open the dashboard — on any device, just once. And nobody 'loses' their points anymore: next to every weekly score you'll now see the all-time total, so Monday's reset clearly starts the race over without taking anything away."

### UI Change Record — 2026-09-16 — AI Models settings becomes a real manager (view/reorder/test/confirm-delete + AI Health feed)
- Added / Changed: `src/components/settings/AiModelsCard.tsx` (rewrite — provider rows now render every model as a chip with **Brain**/**Fallback** role tags and per-chip ↑/↓ reorder arrows that persist the model order via PUT; providers themselves reorder via ↑/↓ arrows that swap both rows' `order` fields (two PUTs); new ⚡ Test button per provider (POST `/api/ai/models`, result line "ok — N models · Xms" mint / error rose); 🗑️ remove now opens the shared portaled `Modal` confirm ("Remove {name}? …This can't be undone." + Cancel / Remove provider footer); new **AI Health** section at the bottom — outcome summary line ("Last N: X ok · Y wrap-up · Z timeout · avg Ss"), last-failure line with reason + relative time, and a recent-outcome dot trail (max 12, emerald ok / sky wrap-up / amber exhausted / rose snag-or-client) with title tooltips + a Refresh button; read-only **env** fallback chain renders as a dashed-border group (amber dot + "read-only · env" chip + Brain/Fallback model chips) so the real answering brain is always visible — the "No brain configured" lie is gone), `src/app/api/ai/providers/route.ts` (GET: `active` now comes from `resolveChatTargets()` — the SAME chain chat uses — and returns `envProviders` read-only pseudo-provider groups whenever the resolved chain includes non-dashboard targets, e.g. the legacy FALLBACK_* env chain; `readOnly: true` marks them), NEW `src/lib/ai/health.ts` (50-entry in-memory outcome ring buffer — metadata only: outcome/rounds/ms/brain/targets/reason, never message content), NEW `src/app/api/ai/health/route.ts` (GET, session-gated like `/api/ai/providers` GET). Chat side: `src/app/api/hermes/chat/route.ts` (`MAX_ROUNDS` 4→6, final round is a forced tool-free wrap-up with an "answer now" system note in both streamed and buffered paths; exhaustion message now fires only when even the wrap-up fails on every target; `callAiStream` drops `tools`+`tool_choice` together when no tools are armed; per-target warnings carry elapsed ms; every request records one health outcome incl. `client_gone` when the stream write channel dies).
- Visual / Motion: No new keyframes, no new loops; the confirm dialog is the shared portaled Modal (spring enter / settle exit) and the AI Health refresh is a plain fetch on tap. `prefers-reduced-motion` untouched.
- Color sources: Existing tokens only — `--color-accent-button` (Brain badge), `--color-accent-mint` (ok test line + Brain chip tag + ok dots), sky/amber/rose chips for wrap-up/exhausted/snag outcomes, `--color-accent-rose` (errors), existing glass surfaces. No new palette entries.
- Agent action required: Update this section + "Current Dashboard Snapshot" + §5.6 route table + "How do I change Consuela's brain?" journey + Change Log. **CONTRACTS to keep:** (1) the wrap-up round never offers tools and never executes tool_calls — `WRAPUP_NOTE` rides as a trailing system message on that round only; (2) `active` + provider visibility must stay derived from `resolveChatTargets()` — never re-derive from the PB table alone or the env-fallback lie returns; (3) health entries are metadata ONLY (no message text/bodies) and the buffer is intentionally volatile (container restart = clean slate; docker logs are durable); (4) read-only env rows never get Edit/Remove/reorder controls; (5) remove always confirms via the shared Modal — no instant delete. Verified: full suite **2148/2148 (295 files)**, tsc clean, eslint 0 on touched files, `next build` clean. **Ops: none** (no new PB collections; no `npm run pb:seed` needed; in-memory health buffer needs nothing).
- User-facing description (copy-paste ready for responses):
  > "Three fixes for the Ask Consuela troubles. First, Consuela now answers even when she has to look up a lot of things — she gets more steps and is now forced to write you an answer with whatever she's found instead of giving up with 'ran out of steps'. Second, Settings → AI Models finally shows you what's set up: which model is the brain, which are fallbacks, and if the brain is coming from an old environment setting you can see that too (marked read-only). You can reorder models with the little arrows (the first one answers), test a provider with the ⚡ button, and removing a provider now asks for confirmation first. Third, there's a new AI Health panel in that same card — it shows how recent chats went (how many were fine, how many hit a timeout, how many ran out of steps) so you'll know at a glance if it's the AI being slow or something timing out."

### UI Change Record — 2026-09-15 — Google multi-day coverage: events now land on EVERY covered day (data correctness — no visual-language change)
- Added / Changed: `src/lib/calendar/google-mapping.ts` (NEW pure coverage core — `googleEventCoveredDays`/`googleEventCoversDay` + `expandGoogleEvent`: every LOCAL day a Google row covers; all-day `end_iso` EXCLUSIVE, a timed end exactly at local midnight belongs to the start day only, missing/invalid/end-≤start degrades to the start day, 366-day cap; date-only `start_iso`/`end_iso` parsed as LOCAL all-day values by SHAPE regardless of the `all_day` flag), `src/lib/consuela/todays-events.ts` (`mergeTodaysEvents`/`mergeEventsRange` filter Google rows by covered day — the `start_iso`-prefix day filter is gone), `src/lib/consuela/live-reads.ts` (`liveGoogleEvents`/`liveEventsRange` field selects carry `end_iso,all_day`), `src/lib/hermes-tools.ts` (`get_todays_events`/`get_calendar_range`/`get_dashboard_summary` merge spanning rows on every covered day), `src/lib/consuela/assistant-context.ts` (planner / "Consuela's week" calendar window now sees spanning events), `src/lib/screensaver/compose.ts` + `payload.ts` (board keeps spanning events through their last day; the Google read stays UNFILTERED behind the 45s payload cache), `src/app/calendar/page.tsx` (month-grid dots, Today panel, Upcoming cards via `flatMap(expandGoogleEvent)` — one mapped row per covered day; flagged single-day rows keep the byte-identical `g_{google_id}_{day}_{month1}_{year}_{time}` id so device caches don't churn). Tests pinned: `tests/unit/calendar/google-mapping.test.ts` (24 — spans, exclusive end, midnight-excluded timed end, date-only-shape-wins red-proofs, legacy id shapes), `todays-events-merge` (9) + `todays-events-range` (3), `hermes-tools-calendar-range` (7) + `hermes-tools-live-reads` (7), `screensaver-compose` (9) + `screensaver-payload` (5) + `screensaver-route` (4) + `screensaver-board` (6), `google-calendar-multical` (12) + `google-calendar-410` (2).
- Visual / Motion: No visual language change — data-correctness only. Nothing new renders, animates, or restyles: an event spanning Oct 30 → Nov 1 simply now shows on each of its days (grid dots, Today/Upcoming, chat answers, the Consuela's-week card, the /screensaver board) instead of only its first. `prefers-reduced-motion` untouched.
- Color sources: None — logic-only change.
- Agent action required: Update this section + "Current Dashboard Snapshot" + Change Log. **CONTRACTS to keep:** (1) ALL Google day-membership goes through `googleEventCoversDay`/`googleEventCoveredDays` ONLY — never re-add a start_iso prefix/`~`-contains filter; (2) all-day `end_iso` is EXCLUSIVE and date-only `start_iso`/`end_iso` are ALWAYS treated as local all-day values by SHAPE (regardless of the `all_day` flag — post-fix contract; accepted edge: a date-only start paired with a timestamped end degrades to the start day); (3) `liveGoogleEvents`/`liveEventsRange` field selects must keep `end_iso,all_day`; (4) the screensaver Google read stays unfiltered behind its 45s cache; (5) single-day flagged rows keep byte-identical `g_{google_id}_{day}_{month1}_{year}_{time}` ids; (6) the calendar page must use `expandGoogleEvent` flatMap. Verified: focused suites green (calendar/ dir, todays-events merge+range, hermes-tools calendar-range+live-reads, screensaver compose/payload/route/board, google-calendar multical+410 — 12 files / 99 tests) + full suite 1905/1905 (272 files), tsc clean. **Ops: none** — the sync backend already stored `end_iso`.
- User-facing description (copy-paste ready for responses):
  > "Trips that cover more than one day now show up on every day they touch. If Google Calendar has something like 'Bailey & Emily at home' running from Friday through Sunday, you'll now see it on Friday, Saturday, AND Sunday — in the month grid dots, the Today and Upcoming panels, when you ask Consuela what's on that day or over the weekend, on her 'Consuela's week' card, and on the wall display board. Before, multi-day events only appeared on the first day and quietly vanished from the rest of the trip."

- Visual / Motion: The sign-in sheet answers an under-10 tap instantly — no PIN pad, "Signing in as …" beat, then the kid's own Home. Chore taps for eligible kids skip the PIN modal entirely and show the existing confetti + "⏳ On the way" pending row (the 2026-09-06 approval UI is reused unchanged; parents' "Needs approval" queue now also collects 10+ kid taps and claims). Settings → Family Members edit/add modal grows one number row under Role, same glass input grammar as the PIN field. No new keyframes, no loops; `prefers-reduced-motion` untouched.
- Color sources: None new — existing tokens only (the Age input reuses the PIN field's `--color-surface-2` + `border-white/10` row).
- Agent action required: Update this section + "Current Dashboard Snapshot" + Change Log. **CONTRACTS to keep:** (1) `PIN_FREE_MAX_AGE` lives ONLY in `src/lib/task-utils.ts` and eligibility is ONLY decided server-side (`/api/auth/quick-login` + the claim route) — the client branch is a UX hint that must fail safe to the PIN modal on any 403; never trust a client-supplied age/flag. (2) The two predicates are the ONLY kid-action seams — `shouldUsePendingTap` is gone (grep `src tests` stays empty); do not re-add an age-blind seam. (3) Kid claims land `pendingApproval` with `sentBackAt: null` (the null clears the stale send-back proof on the tasks COLLECTION row — never drop it) and MUST NOT touch week_data; approve reuses the existing taskId-idempotent flow; `mergeTasksSnapshot`'s sentBackAt proof is TIMESTAMP-GATED — a snapshot stamp only wipes a local pending row when it does not pre-date that row's `pendingApproval.at` (otherwise a send-back → kid re-claim inside one pull window orphans the fresh tap — pinned by `tasks-pending-merge`). (4) Boundary is strict `< 10`: Jasmine (age 10) keeps her PIN — tests pin it; do not "round" the rule. (5) `age` is parent-edited data (adults-only `/api/members/admin`); blank age fails closed to the PIN path and the blank Age field means LEAVE UNCHANGED (key omitted from the PATCH — never send `""`/0; PB `age:0` is rejected by `ageOf`). (6) Copy must describe the age rule only ("Under 10 signs in with one tap") — never name specific children or imply an exact-name allowlist. **Ops on deploy: `npm run pb:seed` (gains members.age) then `npx tsx scripts/consuela/set-member-ages.mjs` ONCE**, then smoke quick-login (Caspian → 200 cookie, Jasmine → 403 pin_required). **Spec deviation (recorded in the final review):** spec §5 row 3 designed 503 for PB-unreachable quick-login; the route ships 403 `pin_required` — a 403 cannot be confused with an outage by the UI and never signs anyone in; the catch-all is pinned by `auth-quick-login-route` tests.
- User-facing description (copy-paste ready for responses):
  > "The little ones got their own front door. Caspian and Aurora (under 10) now sign in with a single tap on their avatar — no PIN — and they can tap their own chores done just as easily. But nobody skips the grown-ups: every kid finish (including any big-kid or shared-chore claims, which still use a PIN) now waits in the parents' 'Needs approval' queue before points move. Ten-and-up kids keep typing their PIN exactly like before — Jasmine's 10th birthday changed nothing — and parents set each person's age in Settings → Family Members, which is what decides who gets the one-tap treatment."

### UI Change Record — 2026-09-09 — Ask Consuela: conversation steering (/new, /restart, New-conversation button)
- Added / Changed: `src/app/api/chat/messages/route.ts` (NEW `POST {action:"reset"}` — session-gated via `verifySession`; writes one `role:"system"`, `content:"New conversation"`, `source:"dashboard"` row into the UTC-daily thread with `createdAt=now`; rejects non-reset actions 400, guests 401), `src/app/chat/page.tsx` (`Message.role` widened to include `"system"`; `fetchPBThread` maps system rows through with no speaker; `startNewConversation()` appends a local system marker optimistically then POSTs the reset best-effort (offline → local divider only); `sendMessage` intercepts `/new` + `/restart` (case-insensitive) AFTER the in-flight guard — ignored while Consuela replies, press stop first; the LLM history slice starts AFTER the newest `role:"system"` marker (`lastIndexOf("system")`) so the model's context resets while the thread keeps the full day; the top-bar button re-pointed from trash/delete to a reset-arrow icon, `aria-label="Start a new conversation"`, `title="New conversation (/new)"`; the confirm Modal is now "Start a new conversation?" with honest steering copy ("Consuela starts fresh… Nothing is deleted…") and a ✨ tip line naming /new + /restart; the destructive rose footer button became the accent "Start new conversation"; system rows render as a centered hairline divider "✨ New conversation" (`role="separator"`, 11px uppercase tracking) and are excluded from bubbles, read-aloud (`lastAssistantReply` filters assistant only), and speaker logic), `src/components/chat/UnifiedInput.tsx` (first-run tip gained "type /new to start a fresh conversation"), tests: NEW `tests/unit/chat-messages-reset.test.ts` (3: guest 401, sessioned marker write shape, invalid-session 401) + NEW `tests/unit/chat-conversation-reset.test.tsx` (5: /new resets without an LLM call, /restart identical, PB system row renders as divider, history cuts at the newest marker — post-reset first send has EMPTY history and the follow-up carries only the post-reset exchange, trash button confirms then resets with a marker POST) + `tests/unit/chat-page-stream.test.tsx` clear-conversation test rewritten to the steering contract.
- Visual / Motion: The top-bar trash icon became a circular reset-arrow (same 32px glass-subtle pill, same 44px+ hit area). A new thread element exists: the conversation divider — a full-width hairline pair with a centered 11px uppercase "✨ New conversation" label between them. No keyframes, no loops; `prefers-reduced-motion` untouched.
- Color sources: divider hairlines `bg-white/10` (matches the chat bubble border alpha); label `text-text-secondary`. No new palette entries.
- Agent action required: Update this section + "Current Dashboard Snapshot" + Common Journeys + Change Log. Contracts to keep: (1) reset markers are `role:"system"` rows in the UTC-daily thread — the LLM cutoff and the divider BOTH key on that; never filter system rows out of the thread read (they must merge across devices); (2) history slicing is stateless (computed from the merged list each send) — no client-side cutoff state to drift; (3) `/new` is intercepted client-side, never sent to the LLM; (4) guests keep a local-only divider (POST 401s by design); (5) `POST /api/chat/messages` accepts ONLY `{action:"reset"}` — do not grow it into a general chat-write endpoint.
- User-facing description (copy-paste ready for responses):
  > "Ask Consuela now has conversation steering, like Telegram or OpenCode. Type /new (or /restart) in the message box — or tap the new-conversation button at the top — and Consuela starts a fresh conversation: she forgets everything before the marker, and a '✨ New conversation' divider shows exactly where the fresh start began. Nothing is deleted; the whole day stays readable above the divider, and the family thread on the server keeps everything too."

### UI Change Record — 2026-09-07 — Ambient wall-display screensaver (/screensaver)
- Added / Changed: `src/lib/screensaver/compose.ts` (NEW pure composer — data in, decisions out: `ScreensaverPayload` type = the entire disclosure surface (date, ≤4 events, dinner, tasks, ≤3 briefing lines, weather); `selectTodayEvents` merges family `events` + Google rows, drops past timed events, all-day first, caps at 4; `choreProgress` = this-week done vs open-due-by-week-end; `briefingDigest` ≤3 honest lines from today's stored briefing; `wxConditionLabel` compact WMO map), `src/lib/screensaver/payload.ts` (NEW impure half — one `withAdmin` session + five PB reads + Open-Meteo; whole-payload cache 45s, weather cache 15 min; PB failure throws → honest 503, weather failure degrades to null; `__resetScreensaverCaches()` test seam), `src/app/api/consuela/screensaver/route.ts` (NEW — GET-only, `force-dynamic`, 503 `{ok:false,error:"db_unreachable"}`, POST → 405), `src/middleware.ts` (`/api/consuela/screensaver` added to `API_EXEMPT` — exact-or-trailing-slash semantics, lookalike siblings stay gated), `src/components/ui/CacheRefresher.tsx` (skips gateway polling + the once-per-load flush on `/screensaver*` — the board owns that page's polling; `mounted` ref re-arms polling on client navigation), `src/app/screensaver/page.tsx` + `src/components/screensaver/ScreensaverBoard.tsx` (NEW `/screensaver` — read-only ambient board: local clock ticking 30s, 60s poll of the exempt endpoint + tab-visible pull, keeps last-good data on failure, quiet amber stale dot after 5 min), `src/lib/consuela/chat-context.ts` (`parseMinutes` exported — the single time-math source), tests: NEW `screensaver-compose` (7) + `screensaver-payload` (4) + `screensaver-route` (4 incl. the no-sensitive-keys scan) + `screensaver-board` (3) + `cache-refresher-screensaver` (3), `middleware-exempt` +2; live probe `scripts/consuela/verify-screensaver.mjs` (guest endpoint shape + chrome + overflow + probe key scan catching token/contact + compound key names).
- Visual / Motion: A new standalone surface, not a restyle — fixed full-bleed dark `#0f1117` canvas (no PageShell, no capsule nav, no Emergency shield, no theme switching), 14vh black tabular clock + date line, three white-alpha glass panels (Today with per-event color dots, Tonight dinner + chore progress bar, Consuela digest) + a weather block; all type in vh so it scales with the wall display. One-shot transitions only, no infinite loops; `prefers-reduced-motion` safe by construction (nothing animates continuously).
- Color sources: Fixed dark canvas `#0f1117` + white-alpha layers only (`bg-white/[0.05]`, `ring-white/10`, `text-white/45–85`) — deliberately theme-independent (a wall display never follows a phone's Accent Studio); event dots carry each event's existing color; progress bar emerald-400/80; stale dot amber-400/80. No dashboard-token changes, no new palette entries.
- Agent action required: Update this section + "Current Dashboard Snapshot" + Change Log. Contracts to keep: (1) `/api/consuela/screensaver` is GET-only + exempt WITHOUT trailing slash (siblings stay gated) — never add write verbs; (2) the payload's six zones are the entire disclosure surface — a future field needs a spec amendment + the no-sensitive-keys test updated deliberately; briefing digest suggestion titles may contain family first names (same tier as the exempt briefing GET) — treat any change to that tier deliberately; (3) `CacheRefresher` skips `/screensaver*` — the board owns that page's polling; (4) `parseMinutes` is now exported from `chat-context` — the single time-math source. Spec: `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-09-07-ambient-screensaver-design.md`; plan: `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-09-07-ambient-screensaver.md`.
- User-facing description (copy-paste ready for responses):
  > "The wall display has a calm mode now. After five minutes of nobody touching it, it fades to the ambient family board — a big clock, the weather, what's on today, what's for dinner, how this week's chores are going, and up to three lines from Consuela's briefing — dimmed for the kitchen. A tap (or a wave, if motion wake is on) brings the live dashboard straight back. Everything on the board is read-only: chores, meals and alerts still need a signed-in family member and their PIN."

### UI Change Record — 2026-09-06 — Tasks: kid tap-to-complete with pending parent approval
- Added / Changed: `src/types/tasks.ts` (`Task.pendingApproval?: { byName, at, points }` — done-but-unpaid marker; no new collections/routes), `src/lib/task-utils.ts` (NEW pure `isPendingApproval(task)` — the only pending gate on the client — + `pendingPointsFor(member, tasks)` per-kid in-flight sums; NEW `approvePendingCompletion(tasks, weekData, taskId)` posts the `earn` tx idempotent by `taskId` (same-week `"Completed: …"`, rolled-week `"Approved: …"`) + NEW `sendBackPendingCompletion(tasks, taskId)` reopens with zero history; `syncTasksToPB` carries `pendingApproval`; `mergeTasksSnapshot` adopts the field on known rows), `src/lib/pb-seed.ts` (`tasks` collection +`pendingApproval` json field — self-heals on next `npm run pb:seed`), `src/app/tasks/page.tsx` (child sessions skip `verifyPinRemote` for assigned non-universal taps — tap/swipe/keyboard set `completed` + real `completedBy/At/InWeek` + `pendingApproval`, confetti + "+Npts on the way" copy, no `earn` tx; pending rows render "⏳ On the way" and disable re-tap; the tapping kid can self-cancel PIN-free; NEW parents-only "Needs approval" `SectionCard` (`role === "parent"`, kid/guest sessions never see it) with per-row kid name + tap date + points and 44px Approve / Send-back buttons opening the shared parent-PIN loop (`verifyPinRemote` per parent, `unreachable` breaks with honest copy, PIN clears both paths; empty-PIN error "Parent PIN required to review tapped tasks."; Approve toasts "Approved!", send-back "Sent back — no points were given."); universal/stealable claims, adult PIN flows, and PIN undo unchanged), `src/modes/kid/KidHome.tsx` (quest "Do it" completes PIN-free into the same pending record for assigned non-universal quests; universal/error paths keep the PIN contract), `src/app/page.tsx` (Home Tasks widget header gains a parents-only "N need approval →" link to `/tasks`, nonzero only), tests: NEW `tasks-pending-approval` + `tasks-pending-merge` + `tasks-pending-flow`, extended `kid-home-quest-pin-safety` + `kid-pin-error-paths` flips + `pb-seed-field-heal`.
- Visual / Motion: Nothing new moves. Pending rows keep the priority-tinted liquid-glass row with the "⏳ On the way" meta line; the approval section reuses the `SectionCard` shell with `glass-subtle` pill buttons; the Home badge is a quiet header line. Confetti on kid tap is the existing celebration. `prefers-reduced-motion` untouched.
- Color sources: None new — existing tokens only (`text-text-secondary` meta, `glass-subtle` pills, `--color-accent-*` rows).
- Agent action required: Update this section + "Current Dashboard Snapshot" + Change Log. Contracts to keep: `isPendingApproval()` is the ONLY pending gate (never re-implement); pending points never enter `weekData.points`/`history` until approve; `verifyPinRemote` stays the only PIN seam (no page-local verifiers); `completedInWeek` keeps the no-double-complete guarantee; regen treats pending as done (reads `completed`); approve is idempotent by `taskId` (double-approve from two devices cannot double-pay). **Approve concurrency contract (2026-09-23):** approvals are server-authoritative via `POST /api/tasks/approve` — idempotent by `taskId+member` under the week-ledger lock; the client remains optimistic and adopts the server `weekData`; offline keeps a local approval that reconciles on the next successful POST. **Ops: run `npm run pb:seed` on deploy** so live PB gains `tasks.pendingApproval` (self-heal). Spec: `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-09-06-tap-to-complete-pending-approval-design.md`; plan: `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-09-06-tap-to-complete-pending-approval.md`.
- User-facing description (copy-paste ready for responses):
  > "Kids can now tap a chore done with no PIN — the task shows done right away with confetti and '+Npts on the way,' but the points only land when a parent approves. Parents see a 'Needs approval' section on the Tasks page (Approve awards the points, Send back reopens the chore with nothing given — both ask for a parent PIN), plus a quiet count on the Home Tasks card. Claiming shared chores, grown-up flows, and undo all work exactly as before."

### UI Change Record — 2026-09-04 — Calendar: glass-month title, day strip, gradient selection
- Added / Changed: `src/app/globals.css` (NEW `@keyframes calendarMonthSettle` (opacity + 8px rise, .24s `--ease-settle`); `.calendar-month-title.is-animating`; NEW day-strip stack — `.calendar-day-strip-wrap` (hidden-scrollbar h-scroller) → `.calendar-day-strip` (flex, `min-width: max-content`) → `.calendar-strip-day` buttons (weekday letter `.wd` 11px over a 30px numeral circle `.num`, min-width 44px touch norm, `.18s` settle transitions) with `.is-today` accent ring and `.is-selected` accent-gradient pill (`--color-accent-button` → 72% color-mix with `#111827`, white numeral, accent glow — the SegmentedControl-emphasize recipe); dark + BOTH light mirror blocks; `.calendar-day-btn.is-selected` gray/blue hardcoded gradient → the same accent-gradient pill + token border/shadow (scale 1.06 kept); light `is-selected.is-today` muted fill → same gradient (both light blocks); reduced-motion kill block gained the `calendarMonthSettle` + strip-transition kills), `src/app/calendar/page.tsx` (month title gains `key={year-month}` + `is-animating` — the settle replays on every month change via key-remount; NEW day strip inside the month-grid card between the header and the weekday row, one `<button>` per day of the month driving the SAME `selectedDay` state the grid cells use — strip selection and grid selection can never disagree; NEW scrollIntoView effect keeps the selected strip day centered on the strip's own horizontal axis, only on the Calendar tab, `behavior: "auto"` under reduced motion), tests: NEW `tests/unit/calendar-glass-refresh.test.tsx` (3: title class + key-remount, strip contract, shared selection), `tests/unit/compact-type-floor.test.tsx` (+2 CSS contract asserts); live probe `/tmp/sdd/probes/calendar-glass.mjs` (19 checks).

Between the header and the weekday row sits a new horizontal strip of every day in the month (weekday letter over a numeral circle); the day you're looking at wears the solid accent-gradient pill with white numeral, today keeps its accent ring, and tapping any day jumps the whole card to it (the strip auto-scrolls to keep the picked day centered). The grid's selected day cell joined the same accent gradient (was a one-off gray-blue gradient). Dark and light render the same component; every new motion is covered by the `prefers-reduced-motion` kill block (verified live: the month-title animation computes to 0s under reduce).

- Agent action required: Update this section + "Current Dashboard Snapshot" + Change Log. Contracts to keep: (1) the strip and the grid share ONE `selectedDay` state — never introduce parallel strip state; (2) the month-title settle replays via React key-remount (`key={year-month}`), not timers — do not restructure the header without keeping the remount; (3) `.wd` is exactly 11px (the type floor) and strip buttons are ≥44px wide — do not shrink; (4) light-theme changes to strip/selected-day rules must be mirrored in BOTH light blocks (`:root[data-theme="light"]` + the `prefers-color-scheme` mirror); (5) every new calendar keyframe/transition goes in the reduced-motion kill block. Spec/plan: `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-09-04-calendar-glass-refresh-design.md` + `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-09-04-calendar-glass-refresh.md`.
- User-facing description (copy-paste ready for responses):
  > "The Calendar page got a friendly polish. A little row of every day in the month now sits right under the month name, so you can tap a day to peek at it without hunting through the grid — the day you pick gets a colorful pill, and the strip slides it into view on its own. The month name also does a gentle little rise-and-settle when you flip months, and the day you select in the big grid now glows in your chosen accent color (it used to be a plain gray-blue). Everything stays calm if you have reduced motion turned on."

- Added / Changed: `src/types/tasks.ts` (`Task.stealable?: boolean`), `src/lib/task-utils.ts` (NEW `isSnatchable(task, today?)` — pure day-precision gate: `stealable && !completed && due < localTodayISO()`; `syncTasksToPB` now writes `stealable`), `src/lib/pb-seed.ts` (`tasks` collection +`stealable` bool field — self-heals on next `npm run pb:seed`), `src/app/api/tasks/claim/route.ts` (claimable = `task.universal !== false` OR (`stealable === true` AND `due < localTodayISO()`); new 400 reason `not_late_yet`; steal completes write the ledger line `Snatched: {title}`, claims unchanged record `Completed: …`; all server-trust guarantees — server-side points, claimed-twice 409 race detection, lost-update re-read — unchanged), `src/app/tasks/page.tsx` (Add/Edit modal gains the "⏰ Up for grabs when late" Toggle; the assignee select, member filter strip, and `startAdd` default all exclude `role === "pet"`; Up-for-grabs + My Tasks filters include `isSnatchable` tasks; stealable-late rows get a "was due {Day}" meta line and open the same claim modal as universal tasks; fresh-week champion card replaced by an honest "👑 The crown is up for grabs / Everyone starts at zero…" state with no 0% ring and no Share; Share is a ghost SoftButton top-right with a real aria-label; Add/Edit assignee select no longer hardcodes Caspian as fallback), `src/components/leaderboard/DailyQuestCard.tsx` (button "Go" → "Do it" + `aria-label="Do {title}"`), `src/app/globals.css` (NEW `.hit-44` utility — `position:relative` + `::before inset:-4px` invisible hit-area expansion, the documented photo-remove pattern shared; applied to row X/undo/✎/⚠️ buttons), tests: NEW `tests/unit/tasks-snatchable.test.ts`, `tasks-snatchable-flow.test.tsx`, `tasks-daily-quest.test.tsx`, `tasks-ai-honesty.test.tsx`, `tasks-champion-zero.test.tsx`, `tasks-polish-copy.test.tsx`, extended `task-claim.test.ts` + `pb-seed-field-heal.test.ts`; probe `scripts/consuela/verify-tasks-polish.mjs` (28 checks, guest-seeded, 390+1280).
- Visual / Motion: Nothing new moves. Stealable rows keep the priority-tinted liquid-glass row; the "was due" phrase lives in the existing meta line. Champion zero-state reuses the Surface + crown. The 768px desktop column is a container cap only — warm-glass world, materials, and the emphasize tab pill are all resident. `prefers-reduced-motion` untouched.

- Agent action required: **Ops: run `npm run pb:seed` on deploy** so live PB gains `tasks.stealable` (self-heal). Contracts to keep: `isSnatchable()` is the ONLY snatch gate on the client (never re-implement string date compares); the claim route's day-precision compare uses the server's local clock (TZ=America/Detroit on the NAS) — never UTC, and the server is the final arbiter (a device in another timezone can differ by a day around midnight; the 400 rollback covers it); `hit-44` is the shared solution for small icon buttons — do not re-varnish per page; the champion card must never crown a 0-point champion. Spec `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-09-04-tasks-polish-and-snatch-design.md`; plan `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-09-04-tasks-polish-and-snatch.md`.
- User-facing description (copy-paste ready for responses):
  > "Tasks got friendlier and fairer. When you add or edit a chore there's a new toggle — 'Up for grabs when late' — and if that chore isn't done by its due date, anyone in the family can grab it and take the points (it pops into the Up for grabs row with a 'was due' note). Fresh weeks no longer pretend somebody is winning at zero points, the numbers at the top follow whichever family member you've filtered to, dogs can't be assigned chores, the daily-quest buttons jump straight to doing the task instead of making a copy, and the little buttons are easier to tap. Consuela also stops suggesting chores when she can't reach the kitchen brain — she says so instead of inventing them."
