corrected to `(subscribe, getClientMembersSnapshot, getServerMembersSnapshot)`), `src/app/settings/page.tsx` (member PATCH/POST/DELETE now `await db.refreshMembersCache()` — which dispatches `consuela-members-updated` — BEFORE the re-read; `db/index.ts` exposes `db.refreshMembersCache()`), `src/components/ui/SyncManager.tsx` DELETED (zero importers, hardcoded fake data). **(3) Home refresh truth:** `src/app/page.tsx` (the Tasks + Daily Schedule reads moved into a `consuela-data-refreshed` listener effect — first read can still fail to `homeError`, refresh failures keep what's shown), `src/db/index.ts` (`refreshCaches` pulls `GET /api/tasks/sync` via new `refreshTasksSnapshot()` in the `Promise.allSettled` batch; browser-only; 401 swallowed — local stays authoritative), `src/lib/task-utils.ts` (ADD-only seam: pure `mergeTasksSnapshot` + `applyTasksSnapshotToStores` store writer; no-change refresh returns inputs by reference). **(4) PIN honesty:** `src/modes/kid/kid-store.ts` (`verifyPinRemote` → discriminated `PinVerifyResult` `{ok | wrongPin | unreachable}` + `unreachableCopy()` mirroring the chat offline/server pattern), `src/modes/kid/KidHome.tsx` (quest claim wrapped in try/catch — a network rejection surfaces honest copy + clears the PIN instead of a silent offline no-op; non-universal branch distinguishes 401 from unreachable), `src/modes/kid/RewardsShop.tsx` (redeem + parent-approval loop surface unreachable vs wrong-PIN; the parent loop breaks on unreachable), `src/app/tasks/page.tsx` (the page-local collapsing helper deleted — ALL SEVEN gates (complete/undo/claim/redeem/penalty/adjust/parent-approval) import the kid-store seam and branch on `result.status`; PIN clears on both paths; parent loops stop on first ok-or-unreachable). **(5) Rewards single-source:** `src/app/tasks/page.tsx` (snapshot POST carries `rewards` + `rewardsUpdatedAt`; restore adopts only a strictly-newer stamp — replaces "longer list wins" so a parent's delete no longer resurrects; Tasks-side reward edits stamp), `src/components/settings/RewardSection.tsx` (save/remove/resetDefaults all `touchRewardsStamp()`), `src/app/rewards/page.tsx` (non-kid modes get a calm "This is the kids' rewards shop" explainer + Tasks link — no redirect, stays discoverable). Tests: +80 (`kid-pin-error-paths` 9, `rewards-delete-truth` 6, `rewards-page-mode-gate` 3, `calendar-members-live-roster` rewritten 4 incl. the unsubscribed-dispatch regression, `task-utils-snapshot-merge` 7, `db-refresh-tasks-snapshot` 3, `home-tasks-schedule-refresh` 3, `google-calendar-multical` 10 incl. both deselect-race red-proofs, `google-pb-collections-heal` 5, `google-calendars-route` 8, `google-sync-state-route` 3, `calendar/google-mapping` +6, `tasks-pin-error-paths` 16).
- Visual / Motion: No new keyframes or loops; `prefers-reduced-motion` untouched. Google-synced events now wear their own calendar's Google color (rows + month dots; uncolored rows keep the existing cyan fallback) instead of one flat member color. The Settings Google card grows a checkbox list with small color dots + per-calendar sync lines. PIN errors change copy only ("Couldn't reach Consuela — check the connection and try again." / "You're offline — …" vs the old blame-the-parent "Wrong PIN"). `/rewards` for adults/guests shows a quiet explainer card instead of an empty-looking shop.
- Color sources: per-calendar `colorRgb` from Google's calendarList feeds `--event-color` via `colorHex` (fallback `eventColorValues.cyan` — the established default, no new palette entries). Settings dots render `colorRgb || var(--color-accent-cyan)`.
- Agent action required: Update this section + "Current Dashboard Snapshot" + Change Log. **Ops on deploy: run `npm run pb:seed`** (or the first request that hits `ensureGoogleCollections` runs the migration + index heal — the dedupe only deletes true `(calendar_id, google_id)` collisions, keeping the first row). Contracts to keep: `kid-store.verifyPinRemote` is THE PIN seam — never re-introduce a page-local helper that collapses 401/5xx/network to null; every rewards writer must `touchRewardsStamp()` (key `consuela-rewards-updatedAt`) or its edits lose LWW; primary Google rows keep the legacy `g_{google_id}_…` id shape (never re-hash them — device caches depend on it); `useSyncExternalStore` consumers pass the CLIENT snapshot as arg 2 and the deterministic server fallback as arg 3 (the calendar chips' swap-back would silently re-pin the roster); future surfaces using `calendar-member-snapshot` get live-priming for free — do not re-add mount-only roster reads. `createCalendarEvent`/`updateCalendarEvent`/`deleteCalendarEvent` remain primary-scoped (editing a non-primary event opens the Google web UI via `eid` — intentional). The Tasks widget's Home re-pull and KidHome's `dataVersion` listener share the one snapshot seam — keep `mergeTasksSnapshot` guards (no-change = same references).
- User-facing description (copy-paste ready for responses):
  > "Three things got honest and one got bigger. Google Calendar now syncs every calendar you pick, not just your main one — in Settings you'll see your calendars with their Google colors and checkboxes, and each synced event on the Calendar page now wears its own calendar's color. The people filter at the top of the Calendar finally shows the real family (it was stuck on a placeholder list). When a PIN doesn't work, Consuela now tells you the truth — 'Couldn't reach Consuela, check the connection' versus 'Wrong PIN' — everywhere from chores to rewards, and a deleted reward stays deleted across devices instead of coming back."

Emergency now reads as one persistent success state with an explicit Done instead of an auto-close.

On glass-* surfaces the hit-area pattern is `::after` negative-inset (`before:` is dead — the material `::before` wins). Modal captures the trigger in the `open` effect (before the panel exists) — do not move that capture into the focus effect. CapsuleNav renders 7 items in both modes (kid swaps House for Rewards) — the 452px `--capsule-scale` denominator assumes it. - Added / Changed: NEW `src/components/ui/wx-tokens.ts` (SKY/INK/GLASS/GLASS_NIGHT + added storm entries — user's SKY had no storm key but WMO 95/96/99 reach the card), NEW `src/components/ui/WxToys.tsx` (`SunOrb`, `CloudPuff` with `style` passthrough, seagull `Birds` with JS reduced-motion flap gate, `SceneLayers`, clay `Sun/Moon/Cloud/Drop/Bolt/Flake/Fog/Wind/Condition` with inline clay styles, `wmoToScene`/`sceneToCondition`), `src/components/ui/WeatherWidget.tsx` (card hero only: sky crossfade stack + world + clay icon above the live temp + glass location chip; card background `skin.skyBottom` → transparent). Deleted scratch `wx-preview` / `wx-widget-preview` routes.

Thunder verified live via mocked-95 screenshot. DayStrip, footer, Details modal, and all copy lines unchanged; `prefers-reduced-motion` neutralizes CSS loops + unmounts the SMIL flap.

Card + modal sky asserts are gradient classes now, not inline `style.background`. `WeatherScene.tsx` is now unused by the widget (kept for now — delete or reuse in a cleanup pass). Hourly tray emoji → clay minis was done for the strip/chips/5-day rows; modal scrubber rain band kept. **Test fix (post-merge):** `stripEndIndex` cap removed → up to 14 strip hours → `idxFromClientX(500)` mapped to index 8 instead of last index 13 in jsdom (no real layout); test tap x changed from 500 to 9999 to always clamp to `hours.length - 1`. Weather 58/58, tsc/eslint clean. **Review-fix contracts (2026-09-06):** (1) card text ink derives from `heroScene` in the `skin` memo — storm=white `#FFFFFF`, everything else slate-800 `#1E293B` — NEVER read `skin.ink` as a night/storm proxy; `skin.night`-keyed TEXT branches were all migrated to `heroScene === "storm"` (only the card `boxShadow` inset still reads `skin.night`, a highlight not text). (2) The SKY storm/night gradients are contrast-CALIBRATED with their inks — changing any stop requires re-checking WCAG AA at the lightest stop for its scene ink. (3) `wxThumbIn` is opacity+scale ONLY — never add a persistent translate (its call sites are in-flow elements). (4) Moon crescent is a CSS `mask-image` radial bite — do not reintroduce a sky-colored overlay disc (background knowledge is the bug). (5) A new touch-consuming control inside an `overflow-x-auto` scroller must scroll its selection into view (see DayStrip's preview effect) — `touchAction: "pan-y"` otherwise hides overflow content on touch. (6) Pin state flows through `setPinned()` (mirrors `previewPinnedRef`) so timers can guard against un-pinning a user answer.
- User-facing description (copy-paste ready for responses):
  > "The weather card got a fresh toy-like look. The background is now a soft sky that changes with the conditions — cream on clear days, lilac when it rains, violet in storms, indigo at night — with drifting clouds, falling rain or snow, lightning flashes in storms, and little seagulls on nice days. A chunky clay icon above the temperature shows the condition at a glance, including a golden lightning bolt for thunderstorms. The hourly strip is now little clay icons per hour instead of the curve, and the Details view matches with the same skies and icons. Everything works exactly as before."

### UI Change Record — 2026-09-04 — Chat honesty + kid-safety: signed-out banner, draft chips, stop control, honest errors
- Added / Changed: `src/app/chat/page.tsx` (renders `SyncStatusBanner` with chat-specific copy in its own shell; quick-action chips call `fillDraft` instead of `sendMessage` — `draft` state + `draftSeq` remount key; name-neutral prompts; `abortRef`/`stopGenerating` wired to `streamConsuelaChat({signal})`; abort-aware catch keeps the partial reply with no error bubble, empty stop writes "Stopped."; honest error copy with offline vs server distinction; `clearChat` behind a confirm `Modal` ("Clear this conversation?" / "this device only… family thread is kept on the home server") with Cancel + rose Clear footer; 10px→11px text floor ×3; `statusLine` truncate clamp; dead speech-recognition stack removed; `UnifiedInput` props now `sendDisabled={isTyping || composerLocked}` + `streaming={isTyping || composerLocked}` + `onStop` + `showTip={userMessageCount === 0}`), `src/components/chat/UnifiedInput.tsx` (new optional props `sendDisabled`/`streaming`/`onStop`/`initialValue`/`showTip`; textarea stays editable when only send is blocked; send button becomes a stop button (`Square` icon, surface-3 bg) while streaming; `aria-label="Message Consuela"`; `autoFocus` when a draft arrives; tip hidden via `showTip`), `src/lib/chat-stream.ts` (`signal?: AbortSignal` option — `AbortSignal.any([external, timeout(300s)])` on fetch, plus an abort listener racing the body read loop with `reader.cancel()` cleanup and an `AbortError`-named rejection so callers can distinguish stop from failure), `src/components/ui/SyncStatusBanner.tsx` (optional `message`/`className` props, default copy unchanged for PageShell consumers).
- Visual / Motion: no new keyframes or loops; the stop button reuses the send button's position (40px circle, surface-3 instead of accent while active); the amber banner appears under the chat top bar when signed out; the confirm modal is the shared portaled Modal (spring enter, settle exit); `prefers-reduced-motion` untouched.
- Color sources: banner reuses the existing amber token pair; stop button `--color-surface-3` fallback; no new palette entries.
- Agent action required: Update this section + "Current Dashboard Snapshot". Contract: `UnifiedInput` drafts are applied by REMOUNT (pass a changing `key` + `initialValue`) — do not add an effect-setState sync. Aborts always reject with `name === "AbortError"` so callers branch on `controller.signal.aborted`. The chat banner copy is intentionally thread-specific; do not reuse the PageShell copy there.
- User-facing description (copy-paste ready for responses):
  > "Ask Consuela got a trust-and-safety pass. If nobody is signed in, the chat now says so — 'this conversation stays on this device' — instead of silently pretending you're in the family thread. The four suggestion chips no longer do things on one tap: they fill the message box as an editable draft (and no longer name a specific kid). While Consuela is answering, you can keep typing your next message and a stop button replaces send — stopping keeps whatever she already said. Errors now say what actually happened (offline vs server trouble) and what to do, and the trash icon asks before clearing, with honest copy that the family thread on the server is kept."

Components that previously worked around the nav overlap by portaling themselves (RecipeModal, PhotoCropEditor) are unaffected.

- Added / Changed: `src/components/ui/WeatherWidget.tsx` — the °F/°C toggle button is removed from the card header row (the row now holds only the location + holiday badge); `conv` is now Fahrenheit identity (`Math.round` — the Open-Meteo fetch always requests `temperature_unit=fahrenheit`, so every call site renders °F); the dead `toC` helper is deleted; `setUnit` is no longer consumed. `src/hooks/useWeather.tsx` — hydration forces `unit: 'F'` regardless of the stored value, and the existing persist effect writes F back to `home-ai-weather-config` (a device that had "C" stored heals on next load). `tests/unit/weather-widget.test.tsx` — the a11y test's toggle-existence assertion is now a "no Switch-to button exists" assertion; a new test seeds a stored `unit: "C"` config and asserts the card hero/H-L AND the Details modal all render °F and the stored config heals to `F`.
- Visual / Motion: The weather card no longer shows the small °F/°C pill at the top-right of the header row; the location row is title-only. Everything else (hero number, H/L, day strip, Details modal with the 24h scrubber, hourly chips, 5-day rows, feels-like) looks identical — just always Fahrenheit. No motion changes; `prefers-reduced-motion` untouched.
- Color sources: None — no palette changes.
- Agent action required: Update this section + "Current Dashboard Snapshot" + Change Log. There is no Celsius path anywhere in the widget now — do not tell users a °C toggle exists; `weather.unit` is always "F".

  > "The weather card is now always Fahrenheit — the little °F/°C button is gone, so it can't get flipped to Celsius by accident, and the Details view shows °F too. Any device that had Celsius saved switches back to Fahrenheit automatically the next time it loads the dashboard."

### UI Change Record — 2026-09-03 — Meals ✨ Generate gains a day-vs-week option sheet
- Added / Changed: `src/components/meals/PlanTab.tsx` — the ✨ Generate button (week nav) now opens the scope sheet instead of firing a whole-week generation: new `generateSheetOpen` state + `onClick={() => setGenerateSheetOpen(true)}`; new in-component empty-slot math (`weekMealKeys` = Set of `${time}-${mealType}` for meals in `activeWeek`, `dayEmpty` = unfilled slots on `activeDay`, `weekEmpty` = unfilled across `weekDays`); `<GenerateScopeSheet>` rendered at the bottom of the component — `onDay` closes the sheet and calls `generateWeeklyPlan(activeWeek, false, [activeDay])`, `onWeek` calls `generateWeeklyPlan(activeWeek, false)` (unchanged week behavior). Consumes the Task 1 hook contract `generateWeeklyPlan(weekOf: string, overwrite = false, days?: string[])` (days scopes the Hermes prompt and filters the returned plan by day) and the Task 2 sheet `src/components/meals/GenerateScopeSheet.tsx` with props `{ open, dayName, dayEmpty, weekEmpty, onDay, onWeek, onCancel }` (built on the shared Modal, so it portals to body with the pinned-footer layout).

Two full-width option cards: "✨ Just {Full day name}" with subtitle "Fills N empty slot(s) on this day" and "🗓️ Whole week" with "Fills N empty slot(s) across Mon–Sun". A day with all four slots filled disables its card ("This day is already full"); a full week disables the week card. Choosing a scope closes the sheet and the Generate button shows the existing spinner; failures still surface in the existing rose error pill. No new keyframes; `prefers-reduced-motion` untouched.

- User-facing description (copy-paste ready for responses):

### UI Change Record — 2026-09-02 — Weather widget round 3: tappable recovery, snow severity, severity-wins decor, 11px floor, tap-to-pin preview
- Added / Changed: `src/components/ui/WeatherWidget.tsx` — (1) the failure banner + "Try again" button gained `pointer-events-auto` (browser evidence showed the button computing `pointer-events: none` inside the content overlay — real taps hit the decorative `wx-fog` layer; the one action a broken card offers was untappable). (7) teach-by-demonstration effect: once per mount after first data (skipped reduced-motion), `setPreviewIdx(6)` at 900ms → revert at 2300ms. `src/components/ui/WeatherSkins.ts` — `HEAVY_SNOW_CODES` (73/75/85/86) + exported `severeFamily()` (`"storm" | "snow" | null`) + `resolveAccent()`; `severeFamily`/`severe` fields on WeatherSkin; new snow day-skin variant (sky → slate `#8B99B5/#7C8BA3`, accent `#7FA8D9`, white snow particles). `src/lib/weather-insights.ts` — `snowAdvice()` ("Big snow today — boots by the door" / kid "Big snow! Boots and mittens today") sharing the clearing-time detail with `stormAdvice` (extracted `clearingDetail`). `tests/unit/weather-widget.test.tsx` — `vi.hoisted` seam mocks `db.selectTodaysEvents` (module-private cache unreachable in unit tests); +7 tests: snow advisory + slate sky, tappable Try-again class, tap-pin → no auto-revert → Back-to-now release, fusion pill promotion + wear cede, heavy-snow skins ×4 codes, resolveAccent severity-wins, snowAdvice copy.

A snow day reads as serious — slate sky, ice-blue accent, "❄️ Big snow today — boots by the door · Clearing by around 1 PM" — with the same calm authority as storms, and Halloween decorations stand down while the weather is severe. Every remaining text element on the card clears 11px. Tapping the strip pins "7 PM" on the card (bigger thumb, Back-to-now chip) so the answer survives a glance away; on first load the card briefly demonstrates the gesture by itself.

Snow days now get the same serious treatment as storms — slate-gray sky, a 'Big snow today — boots by the door' note, and when it should clear. Halloween decorations politely step aside when severe weather hits. Everything on the card is at least 11px so it's readable across the kitchen. And the best trick is easier to use: tap the temperature curve to pin a future hour on the card (it stays until you tap 'Back to now'), and on first load the card shows you how."

### UI Change Record — 2026-09-02 — Weather widget: Consuela night re-tint, storm severity at a glance, calendar fusion, trust/a11y hardening
- Added / Changed: `src/components/ui/WeatherSkins.ts` — (1) **Consuela's night**: the near-black `#060609→#141420` Not Boring night skin re-tinted to lamplit indigo `#1B1E33→#2A2440` (soft `#2E3350` clouds, `rgba(255,255,255,0.16)` border, 0.66 inkSoft) so the hero stays visible on a dark-kitchen wall; white numerals and condition accents unchanged. (2) **Day-side severity modifier**: `getWeatherSkin(season, isNight, code)` desaturates the pastel day sky toward storm gray (`mixHexColor` 62% toward `#9AA3AE/#7E8794`) and switches to storm amber `#FFB44F` when `code >= 95`; skin carries `severe` + `skyGradient(season|null)`. (3) `skyGradient` is now the single source of the five sky washes. `src/components/ui/WeatherScene.tsx` — sky layers render from `skin.skyGradient()` instead of hardcoded duplicated gradients; the night moon rides the real arc opposite the sun (`1 - sunProgress`, 0.8s eased) instead of sitting pinned at 76%/16%. `src/lib/weather-insights.ts` (NEW, pure + unit-tested) — `wearAdvice` (coat/jacket/water-bottle/sunglasses + raincoat override, kid-aware copy), `stormAdvice` (reassurance-first storm pill with clearing time), `fusionOutlook` (next ≥50% precip hour matched to today's family events ±75 min; parses "16:00" and "4:00 PM"; fallback = reassurance "should pass before {event}"). `src/components/ui/WeatherWidget.tsx` — **a11y**: card `role="img"` → `role="group"` + aria-label (screen readers can now reach the unit toggle, day strip, and Details); hero-wide live region replaced by one sr-only `role="status"` announcing on data refreshes only; modal focus trap (Tab cycles, focus lands on Close, returns to Details trigger via `closeDetails`); Close button 44px; unit toggle + Details ≥44px hit areas with `relative z-30`; toggle shows the target unit ("°C" when in F) as an action; precip chips conditionally rendered (was `opacity:0` ghost announcements); day-strip labels 8.5→10px, scrubber 9→10px, kicker 9→11px; dead chevron rotation removed. **Honest data**: all fabricated fallbacks (`?? 60`/`?? 50`/`?? 25`/`?? 5`) replaced with null → "—", hidden rows, or dropped curve points; hourly temps without values are skipped before entering the strip; `deriveOutlookInfo` returns `{sentence, hitISO}` + storm escalation "Thunderstorms expected around X" + `stormEndISO` (first non-storm hour after the current storm); storm pill renders `⛈️ {headline} · {detail}`; empty 5-day view gets a real message. **Resilience**: 12s AbortController timeout (aborts treated as background failures — no banner spam, guard released); hourly/sunrise/sunset naive ISO parsed via `utc_offset_seconds` → normalized to true UTC (timezone-travel-proof); banner gained a tappable "Try again" → `loadWeather()`. **Fusion**: `familyEvents` from `db.selectTodaysEvents()` (best-effort, re-read on `consuela-data-refreshed`); `wearLine` under H/L; `fusionLine` ("Rain around Soccer Practice") above the outlook. `src/app/globals.css` — reduced-motion kill block gained `weatherConfettiFall`/`weatherShamrockFloat`/`weatherHarvestFloat`/`weatherSnowAccumulate` (holiday particles previously fell forever for reduced-motion users). Deleted dead `src/components/weather/` (particles/icons/StatPill/helpers/backdrops/overlays — zero importers). Tests: `tests/unit/weather-widget.test.tsx` +18 (storm sky + copy, clearing announcement, retry flow, honest-null hero, abort-timeout refetch, role=group/sr-only live region, skins ×3, insights ×4; AuthProvider added to the harness). Probes: `[role="img"]`→`[role="group"]`, night `rgb(27,30,51)`, `data-testid="wx-hero-temp"`, strip `scrollIntoViewIfNeeded` before press.
- Visual / Motion: The night card is now a warm lamplit indigo that reads as part of the bento instead of a black hole. When thunderstorms hit during the day, the pastel sky desaturates to a serious storm gray with an amber accent and a reassurance pill — "⛈️ Thunderstorms — inside is best right now · Clearing by around 6 PM" — appears under the hero. The hero line answers the family question directly ("Raincoats ready", or for kids "Bring a raincoat") and, when rain lines up with a family event, the card says "Rain around Soccer Practice" — something only Consuela can say. The moon now rises where the sun set and tracks the arc through the night. Every control is comfortably tappable, the details dialog traps focus properly, screen readers can reach every control, and no data is ever invented when the API degrades.
- Color sources: NIGHT_SKIN `#1B1E33/#2A2440/#2E3350`; storm modifier derives from each season's own pastel mixed toward `#9AA3AE/#7E8794` with the existing storm amber `#FFB44F`. No new palette entries.
- Agent action required: Update this section + "Current Dashboard Snapshot" + Change Log. Note: the detector's 2 `bounce-easing` warnings (WeatherWidget.tsx:902/1180) are the documented project-standard `--ease-spring` glass-settles token — intentional, do not "fix".
- User-facing description (copy-paste ready for responses):
  > "The weather card got a big care package. At night it's now a soft lamplit indigo instead of pitch black, so you can still read it in a dark kitchen. When thunderstorms roll in during the day, the card turns serious — gray sky, amber accent, and a little note telling you when it should clear up. It now speaks family: 'Raincoats ready' when it's wet, 'Grab a coat' on cold mornings, and when rain lines up with something on your calendar it says 'Rain around Soccer Practice.' Under the hood it stopped inventing data when the weather service misbehaves (honest dashes instead of fake numbers), retries properly with a tappable 'Try again', survives timezone travel, and every button is easier to tap and screen-reader friendly."

### UI Change Record — 2026-08-31 — Weather card polish: wind arrow physics, feels-like on the card, chip semantics
- Added / Changed: `src/components/ui/WeatherWidget.tsx` — (1) **Wind arrow points where the wind blows** (the (Not Boring) "Physics" signature): the modal's Wind row gained a small accent-colored arrow glyph inside the metric pill, rotated to the flow direction (`windDir + 180 % 360` — meteorological direction is where wind comes FROM, the arrow shows where it goes TO), settling over 0.6s as the 24h scrubber moves so the arrow swings through the day. (2) **Feels-like on the card**: the hero's `H:83° L:68°` line now appends `· Feels like 72°` when the apparent temperature differs from the actual by ≥2° (°C-aware via `conv`); hidden while previewing an hour and when the values match — the modal's Feels-like row is unchanged. (4) **Hourly chips keep button semantics**: each hour chip was a `<button role="listitem">` — the role override stripped native button semantics from screen readers (announced as "list item"); the `role="listitem"` moved to a shrink-0 wrapper div so the inner control is a real button inside a proper list (the redesign probe's `[role="list"] button` selector keeps matching). `src/app/globals.css` — dead `wxHeroIn` keyframe removed (defined but never referenced) along with its reduced-motion kill entry. `tests/unit/weather-widget.test.tsx` (+3 tests: feels-like note shown at ≥2° delta / hidden when values match, wind arrow rotated to flow direction).

In the details view the Wind pill gained a small arrow that physically points where the wind is blowing, rotating smoothly as you scrub through the day. The dev-note footer line is gone. No palette or scene changes; `prefers-reduced-motion` untouched (the arrow's rotate is a state transition, covered by the existing transition-neutralizing block).

Also removed a stray 'Tip:' line at the bottom of the details view."

### UI Change Record — 2026-08-27 — Weather scene hydration mismatch fixed (moon path)
- Added / Changed: `src/components/ui/WeatherWidget.tsx` — the card scene's `timestamp` fallback was `new Date().getTime()` at render time; during SSR (weather data not yet fetched) the server computed the moon phase a few ms earlier than the client's first render, so `moonPathD` emitted a terminator radius differing in the 8th decimal (`15.99998662…` vs `15.99998664…`) → React hydration mismatch on `[data-testid="wx-moon-lit"]`. Fix: the pre-data fallback timestamp is now the deterministic constant `0` (`activeHour ? hourTime : weatherData ? Date.now() : 0`) — server HTML and client first render are byte-identical; the real timestamp (and true moon phase) lands with the client-side fetch a moment later. The cloud seed was already hour-quantized and unaffected; `sunProgressAt` already returns a fixed 0.5 pre-data; the modal scene is client-only and untouched.
- Visual / Motion: No visual change — the pre-data frame (a fraction of a second) shows the deterministic fallback scene; the true moon phase appears with the data, exactly as before, minus the console error.
- Color sources: None — logic-only fix.
- Agent action required: Update this section + Change Log. Convention: any scene value derived from wall-clock time must be deterministic (constant or quantized) until client-only data lands.
- User-facing description (copy-paste ready for responses):
  > "Fixed a console error on the weather card: the moon's shape was being computed slightly differently between the server and your browser (milliseconds apart), which React flagged as a mismatch. The card now renders identically on both and the real moon phase appears the instant the weather data loads. Nothing looks different — the error is just gone."

### UI Change Record — 2026-08-27 — Weather scene: birds circle the sun on clear days — seagull flap + pass behind the sun
- Added / Changed: `src/components/ui/WeatherScene.tsx` (`BIRDS` config — 3 birds with elliptical flight amplitudes (`a` 44/58/50px horizontal, `b` 12/16/14px vertical), periods 17/23/20s, negative delays −3/−11/−16s, sizes 10/8/9px, flap rates 0.9/1.1/1.0s, static fallback positions — plus a `birdsOut` gate: day + `cloudCover < 30` + no rain/snow/storm codes + `fogOpacity === 0` + animated. The sun branch carries a `data-testid="wx-birds"` layer inside the sun element, so the flock tracks the sun arc and timeline scrubbing automatically. **Flight:** each bird flies a flattened ellipse via `wxBirdOrbit` — crossing left→right in front of the sun (0–50%, fully visible), then swinging up and around behind the disc where it fades to opacity 0 and scales down to 0.8 (50–90%, the "behind the sun" half), re-emerging at the left side (90–100%). **Flap:** each bird is a two-arc seagull silhouette (`skin.ink` stroke at 55% opacity) whose wing path morphs between wings-up and wings-down shapes via SMIL `<animate attributeName="d">` (spline-eased, works in every browser — no CSS `d` dependency); the container fades over 1.2s as conditions change; reduced motion renders static silhouettes at fixed positions with no animate element), `src/app/globals.css` (`wxBirdOrbit` keyframes — elliptical translate + behind-sun fade + depth scale, driven by per-bird `--wx-bird-a`/`--wx-bird-b` vars; added to the reduced-motion kill block; the earlier scaleY `wxBirdFlap` was removed in favor of the SMIL path morph), `tests/unit/weather-widget.test.tsx` (mock payload gained `cloud`/`code` overrides; +3 tests: birds visible on a clear day, hidden under 90% cloud cover, hidden when raining), `scripts/consuela/verify-weather-features.mjs` (+5 checks: birds hidden under 90% cover, visible on clear sky, three birds, all on the wxBirdOrbit flight path, three SMIL flap animates — probe now 16/16).
- Visual / Motion: On clear days three small seagulls circle the sun the way distant gulls do — flapping steadily (wings morphing up and down), crossing in front of the sun from left to right, then disappearing as they swing behind it and fading back in on the other side. They only appear when the sky is genuinely clear (low cloud cover, no precipitation, no fog) and fade away when clouds roll in. Flights are slow (17–23s per circuit), flaps quick (~0.9–1.1s); under `prefers-reduced-motion` the birds remain as static silhouettes.
- Color sources: bird strokes use `skin.ink` at 55% opacity — correct on every seasonal day skin automatically; night excluded by the gate.
- Agent action required: Update this section + "Current Dashboard Snapshot" + Change Log.
- User-facing description (copy-paste ready for responses):
  > "On clear days, three small seagulls now fly around the sun — flapping their wings like gulls seen from far away. They cross in front of the sun from left to right, then disappear as they swing behind it and reappear on the other side. When clouds roll in (or rain, fog, or sunset), they're gone — they only come out when the sky is genuinely clear."

### UI Change Record — 2026-08-27 — Weather scene: realtime visibility fog, procedural tap-to-puff clouds, true moon phases
- Added / Changed: `src/components/ui/WeatherScene.tsx` (the three (Not Boring) signature features: **(1) Realtime visibility fog** — `SceneState` gained `visibility: number | null` + `timestamp: number`; fog opacity now derives from real visibility meters (≥8km = none; scales to 0.55 as visibility → 0), WMO fog codes 45/48 still trigger, and the old humidity≥82% heuristic survives only as a weak 0.22 fallback when visibility data is missing. **(2) Procedural clouds** — the 4 hardcoded ellipse clusters are replaced by seeded generation: exported `mulberry32` PRNG + `makeCloudSpec(seed, fullness)` build each cloud as 4–9 overlapping ellipses (count/size grow with cloud cover, normalized into a 200×70 viewBox); the seed is the hour timestamp (`floor(ts/3600000) * 31 + slot * 101 + 7`), so each hour of each day has unique clouds, stable within the hour, and scrubbing the timeline morphs the sky to that hour's clouds via a 1.1s `wxFadeIn` crossfade (the previously-unused keyframe). **Tap the clouds to build your own:** each visible cloud handles `pointerdown` and puffs a new blob at the tap x-position — `wxPuff` 0.9s grow-and-fade on an ellipse with `transform-box: fill-box`, max 3 concurrent puffs, cleared on animationend, disabled under reduced motion. **(3) True moon phases** — exported `moonPhase(timestamp)` (synodic model: 29.53058867-day month from the Jan 6 2000 18:14 UTC new-moon epoch → phase 0..1 + illumination fraction + waxing flag), `moonPhaseName(phase)` (8 phase names), and `moonPathD(cx, cy, r, phase)` (terminator path: semicircle limb + half-ellipse terminator, waxing lit right / waning lit left); the night branch now renders the real illuminated fraction over a faint earthshine disc, with glow alpha scaling with illumination), `src/components/ui/WeatherWidget.tsx` (Open-Meteo fetch gained `visibility` on current + hourly; `HourPoint`/`WeatherData` carry `visibility: number | null`; both scene states (card + modal) pass `visibility` and the active/scrubbed hour's `timestamp` — previewing or scrubbing changes the clouds and moon for that hour; the card content overlay became `pointer-events-none` with `pointer-events-auto` on the °F/°C toggle, day strip, and Details button so clouds are tappable (modal hero overlay likewise); the modal Daylight card gained a dotted-leader **Moon** row — "Waxing Gibbous · 78%" — via the new exports), `src/app/globals.css` (NEW `wxPuff` keyframe + added to the `prefers-reduced-motion` kill block), `tests/unit/weather-widget.test.tsx` (+11 tests: moon model at known epoch/full/quarter + 8 phase names, cloud spec determinism/uniqueness/viewBox-bounds/fullness, visibility fog renders at 800m and not at 16km, night scene renders the moon-phase path; mock payload now includes visibility), `scripts/consuela/verify-weather-features.mjs` (NEW — deterministic 11-check Playwright probe with mocked Open-Meteo responses)
- Visual / Motion: The scene now simulates the actual weather instead of a interpretation of it: fog rolls in when real visibility drops (not when humidity guesses say so), every hour has its own hand-built clouds — thicker and wider as cover rises — and tapping a cloud puffs a fresh blob into it where you touched (the (Not Boring) toy, harmless and state-driven). At night the moon is no longer a painted crescent: it shows tonight's true phase — the actual illuminated slice with a faint earthshine disc — and the Daylight card names it ("Full Moon · 100%"). Scrubbing the timeline regenerates that hour's clouds with a soft 1.1s crossfade. All motion is CSS-only and neutralized under `prefers-reduced-motion` (the tap toy included).
- Color sources: unchanged — skin palettes from `WeatherSkins.ts`; cloud blobs keep `skin.cloud`/`skin.cloudDeep`, moon lit path keeps `skin.celestial`.
- Agent action required: Update this section + "Current Dashboard Snapshot" + Change Log.
- User-facing description (copy-paste ready for responses):
  > "The weather card now simulates the real weather, not a cartoon of it. Fog appears based on actual visibility distance. The clouds are generated fresh for every hour — thicker when it's cloudier — and you can tap a cloud to puff a new one into it. At night, the moon shows its real phase (tonight's actual illuminated shape), and the details view names it ('Full Moon · 100%')."

### UI Change Record — 2026-08-27 — Weather card polish: timeline label collisions fixed, graphs named, Not Boring fidelity pass
- Added / Changed: `src/components/ui/WeatherWidget.tsx` — (1) **Label collision fix:** the day strip and modal scrubber labeled every 3-hour-aligned hour regardless of distance from the NOW marker, so whenever the current hour preceded a multiple-of-3 hour (e.g. 8:30 AM → NOW at 8AM + "9 AM" one step later) the two labels cramped (strip: 26px apart; scrubber: overlapping at 2px). New shared `pickLabelIndices(hours, x, minGap)` builds the label set with a minimum pixel gap (44px strip / 48px scrubber) — time-of-day-proof by construction. (2) **Compact Not Boring ticks:** new `formatHourTick()` renders "9AM"/"12PM" (no space) on strip, scrubber, and hourly chips; the spaced `formatHourLabel` remains for spoken `aria-valuetext`, chip aria-labels, condition lines, and sun-arc times. NOW tick + strip hairline now render in the state accent (was neutral track color). (3) **Graphs named:** tracked-uppercase kickers — "Rest of today" above the card day strip (`skin.inkSoft`), "Next 24 Hours" above the modal scrubber, "Daylight" inside the sun-arc card; hourly section header renamed "Next 24 Hours" → "Hourly" so the phrase isn't duplicated. (4) **Not Boring fidelity:** modal hourly chips lost borders/backgrounds for open columns — active hour marked by a 2.5px accent top bar + 12% accent tint, inactive chips chrome-less; precip % always rendered but `opacity: 0` below 20% (was a stray "·" glyph); chip gap 8→4px, radius 16→12px. Modal hero numerals 54→60px; card "°" 24→28px and modal "°" 20→24px so the degree mark carries big-number presence.
- Visual / Motion: The timelines now read calm and evenly spaced at every hour of the day, and each graph announces itself ("Rest of today", "Next 24 Hours", "Daylight") instead of asking to be guessed. The modal's hourly row is quieter — chrome removed, accent reserved for the selected hour — one step closer to (Not Boring)'s minimal-chrome grammar. No motion changes; no palette changes.
- Color sources: unchanged — skin accents from `WeatherSkins.ts`.

### UI Change Record — 2026-08-27 — Weather card redesigned: "(Not Boring), Consuela-style" (data-true scene + timeline spine)
- Added / Changed: `src/components/ui/WeatherWidget.tsx` (full rewrite — the static seasonal-illustration card with protruding corner icon is replaced by a data-true scene card: centered huge temperature (56–64px SF Pro weight-900, `tracking-[-0.03em]`, tabular-nums, 550ms cubic count-up on any change), condition + H/L + next-hours outlook beneath, and a new bottom **day strip** — rest-of-day temp curve (midpoint-quadratic smoothed SVG path measured to real width via ResizeObserver) with accent rain ticks (≥40% precip, opacity scaled by probability), NOW + 3-hour labels, and press/drag/keyboard (`role="slider"`, arrows + Escape/Home, `aria-valuetext` speaks hour/temp/precip) preview: the hero temp, condition ("7 PM · Clear"), and the whole scene follow the thumb; release animates back to now after 650ms. The Details modal is rebuilt as a timeline machine: live scene + huge temp for the scrubbed hour, a 24h **TimelineScrubber** (`role="slider"`, rain band, drag + arrows/Home/End, spoken valuetext), exploded **dotted-leader rows** (Humidity/Wind + cardinal/Precipitation/Cloud cover for the scrubbed hour; Feels like/UV 5-dot scale/Pressure only at NOW — missing data hides rows), **SunArc** (quadratic arc, sun dot at real sun progress, sunrise/sunset labels + "Xh Ym of daylight"), and an Hourly|Daily tab toggle (24 scrollable hour chips with emoji/temp/precip ↔ the preserved 5-day range-bar list). The °F pill became a real unit-toggle button wired to `setUnit`; the old animated corner icons, StatPill bento, and bordered hero scrim are gone), `src/components/ui/WeatherSkins.ts` (NEW — the skin system: four pastel day skins auto-keyed to season — spring pastel lemon `#FDF0C4`→lilac `#E9DFF9` w/ blossom accent, summer pastel guava `#FFD8CB`→peach `#FFEBCF` w/ guava accent, autumn soft-red `#FFDCC8`→`#F5BCA0`, winter ice blue `#D9EAFB`→silver `#F4F8FD` — near-black ink on pastel; plus the full Not Boring night skin keyed to real API `is_day`: `#060609→#141420` sky, white ink, one condition accent — storm amber / rain cyan / snow ice / fog gray / default soft red; `cardinalFromDegrees` helper), `src/components/ui/WeatherScene.tsx` (NEW — the data-true scene: five stacked sky layers crossfading via 1.1s opacity transitions, sun disc riding the real sun arc (left/top from sunrise→sunset progress, 0.8s eased) or crescent moon + twinkling stars at night, 4 cloud slots whose count/opacity track `cloud_cover` and drift speed tracks wind, rain streaks (count from precip probability, slant from wind speed/direction) or swaying snow, humidity/fog-code fog banks, lightning flash overlay on storm codes; JS `prefers-reduced-motion` gate freezes all particle motion), `src/components/ui/WeatherParticles.tsx` (NEW — the existing particle system extracted verbatim, now rendered only for active holidays), `src/components/ui/WeatherSeasonArt.tsx` (new opt-out `backdrop` prop — holiday overlays now layer on top of the data-true scene without the old seasonal backdrops), `src/app/globals.css` (NEW `.wx-sky` crossfade class + keyframes `wxCloudDrift`, `wxStarTwinkle`, `wxFogDrift`, `wxRainStreak`, `wxSnowFall`, `wxLightning`, `wxFadeIn`, `wxThumbIn`, `wxHeroIn` + matching `prefers-reduced-motion` kill block), `tests/unit/weather-widget.test.tsx` (rewritten — 7 tests: modal open/close + daily toggle, H/L, strip slider + rain ticks, keyboard preview + return-to-now, night skin via `is_day=0`, modal scrubber valuetext + UV dots), `scripts/consuela/verify-weather-redesign.mjs` (NEW — 30-check Playwright probe), `DESIGN.md` (NEW — dashboard world + weather-card world), `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-08-27-not-boring-weather-redesign-design.md` (direction contract recorded)
- Visual / Motion: The card now reads like (Not Boring) Weather in Consuela's palette — one huge number over a living sky instead of an illustration with an icon in the corner. Every scene element is data-true: clouds thicken as `cloud_cover` rises, rain falls slanted by the real wind, fog banks roll in above 82% humidity, storms flash, and the sun sits where the API says it is on its arc. Day skies are soft pastels (guava in summer) with near-black numerals; after real sunset the card goes full Not Boring — near-black sky, white numerals, a single condition accent. Pressing the day strip previews any hour of the rest of the day: the number counts to that hour's temp, the condition labels the hour, and the sky morphs to match (preview tonight and the card darkens); releasing glides back to now. The modal scrubs the whole next 24 hours the same way — scrub past sunset and the modal's sky darkens too. Skies crossfade (1.1s), temps count up (550ms ease-out), thumbs pop in with a blur-settle; everything is CSS-only and everything stops under `prefers-reduced-motion`. Holidays still overlay the scene (bats, lights, fireworks) with their own particles.
- Color sources: Skin palettes in `WeatherSkins.ts` (pastel season skins + Not Boring night); holiday accents reused from the previous holiday themes. No dashboard-token changes.

Instead of a painted background with an icon in the corner, the card now shows a living sky — the sun sits where it really is, clouds thicken as cover rises, rain falls slanted by the real wind, and after sunset the card turns into a calm dark night view with one accent color. The temperature is one big number in the middle. At the bottom, a little graph shows the rest of the day's temperatures with rain marks — press and slide along it to preview any hour (the sky and temperature follow your finger), and it glides back to now when you let go. The Details view now has a 24-hour timeline you can scrub, humidity/wind/rain rows, a UV dot scale, and a sunrise-to-sunset arc, plus hourly and 5-day views."

Known pre-existing concerns surfaced during verification (NOT plan regressions — baseline-verified): (1) CapsuleNav can overlap inline-Modal footers because PageShell `<main class="relative z-10">` creates a stacking context that traps `z-[80]` modals under the `z-50` nav (RecipeModal portals to body and is unaffected); (2) grocery items with category `other` render nowhere in ShopTab (`groceryCategories` has no `other` entry — same hole in baseline); (3) `/meals` logs one hydration mismatch per guest load — server SSR uses the PB-warmed members cache while the guest client hydrates with fallbacks in the PlanTab member strip (baseline MealsTab had the identical pre-mount `db.selectMembers()` pattern).

A little stepper card at the top always shows which step you're on, and you can collapse it once you know the way. Recipes now live inside Plan as a fold-out box. And nothing lands on your grocery list by surprise — both 'add missing meal ingredients' and 'add low pantry items' show you a preview first, and only add what you confirm."

- Added / Changed: `src/lib/ha/config.ts` (NEW — HA config from env; HA_TOKEN required), `src/lib/ha/websocket-client.ts` (NEW — WebSocket client with reconnect, subscriptions, service calls), `src/lib/ha/rest-client.ts` (NEW — REST client with domain filtering + auth), `src/lib/ha/persist.ts` + `src/lib/ha/bridge.ts` (NEW — entity state → PocketBase `ha_entities` + server bridge `startHABridge()`), `src/lib/mqtt/client.ts` (NEW — optional Zigbee2MQTT MQTT tap), `src/app/api/ha/sync/route.ts` + `src/app/api/ha/call-service/route.ts` + `src/app/api/ha/health/route.ts` (NEW — POST /api/ha/sync, POST /api/ha/call-service, GET /api/ha/health), `src/components/ha/HomeSecurityWidget.tsx` / `HomeClimateWidget.tsx` / `HomeLightsWidget.tsx` (NEW Home widgets), `src/components/ha/{RoomCard,RoomSheet,SecurityPanel,ClimateCard,LightsGrid}.tsx` (NEW House-tab components), `src/app/ha/page.tsx` (NEW — "Home Controls" page with Overview/Security/Climate/Lights/Automation tabs), `src/components/ui/CapsuleNav.tsx` (7th item "House" between Calendar and Settings; hidden for child role — kid mode keeps 6 tabs), `src/lib/layout-config.ts` + `src/app/page.tsx` + `src/app/settings/page.tsx` (homeSecurity/homeClimate/homeLights widget wiring — visible in Settings → Layout & display), `src/instrumentation.ts` (starts `startHABridge()` — env-guarded: HA_HOST/HA_TOKEN required, MQTT_BROKER/MQTT_USER/MQTT_PASS optional), `src/lib/pb-seed.ts` + `pb_migrations/20260821_create_ha_collections.js` (new ha_entities/ha_areas/ha_devices/ha_automations collections), `.env.example` (HA_HOST, HA_TOKEN, MQTT_BROKER, MQTT_USER, MQTT_PASS)
- Visual / Motion: New House tab in the capsule — a house icon with a signal dot that sits between Calendar and Settings and expands into the same neon-lime pill when active (kids never see it — the capsule stays 6 tabs for the child role). The `/ha` page is a full "Home Controls" surface: Overview (RoomCards per area + a RoomSheet for details), Security (prominent Arm home / Disarm panel with status), Climate (ClimateCard thermostat), Lights (LightsGrid toggles), and Automations tabs. On Home, three new widget cards follow the existing pastel bento language (protruding corner icon + tone glow): Home Security (🛡️), Home Climate (🌡️), Home Lights (💡). Without HA credentials the tab and widgets render graceful empty/error states (no fake data). All motion is CSS-only; `prefers-reduced-motion` untouched.

- User-facing description (copy-paste ready for responses):
  > "There's a new House tab in the bottom bar (between Calendar and Settings) that turns the dashboard into your home controls: an Overview of every room, a Security screen with a big Arm home / Disarm switch, Climate with your thermostat, Lights with quick toggles, and your Automations. The Home screen also gained three cards — Home Security, Home Climate, and Home Lights — that you can show, hide, or reorder in Settings → Layout & display. It's powered by your Home Assistant (and optionally Zigbee2MQTT) with the token kept safely on the server, and kids' screens stay just as calm as before — the House tab is hidden for them."

### UI Change Record — 2026-08-26 — Google Calendar sync month-navigation bug fixed (month-aware events)
- Added / Changed: NEW `src/lib/calendar/google-mapping.ts` (pure, unit-tested: `parseGoogleStart` — all-day date-only strings parsed as local dates so US timezones don't shift the day back one; `mapGoogleEvent` — Google row → CalEvent keeping day + month + year, id now `g_{google_id}_{day}_{month}_{year}_{time}`; `eventInMonth` — events without month/year repeat every month, dated events match only their own month/year), NEW `tests/unit/calendar/google-mapping.test.ts` (9 tests), `src/app/calendar/page.tsx` (`CalEvent` gained optional `month`/`year`; `syncGoogleEvents` rewritten to use `mapGoogleEvent` and NO LONGER filters events to the current month — the backend serves a 30-days-back / 90-days-forward window and all of it is kept; dedupe key now includes month/year; `dayEventMap`, `selectedEvents`, `upcomingByDay` filter through `eventInMonth(e, month, year)` so the grid dots, Today panel, and Upcoming panel only show each event in its own month)
- Visual / Motion: No styling change. Behavior fix: before, Google events were mapped to day-of-month only and filtered to the current month at sync time — so (1) navigating to next month showed none of the synced Google events that exist there, and (2) this month's Google events ghosted onto the same day numbers of every other month. Now each Google event appears exactly in its own month; family-created events (no month/year) keep their existing repeats-every-month behavior. Backend (`src/lib/google/calendar.ts` + `/api/google-calendar`) audited and unchanged — it already stores real `start_iso` dates across the 30d-back/90d-forward window.
- Color sources: None — logic-only change.
- Agent action required: Update this section + Change Log. Note: old localStorage Google events (pre-fix id format, no month) still repeat every month until the next sync replaces them — one auto-sync on mount clears them (member==="Google" rows are dropped and re-mapped).
- User-facing description (copy-paste ready for responses):
  > "The Google Calendar sync now handles months correctly. Before, synced events were stamped with just a day number, so they'd copy themselves onto the same day of every month, and events in other months never appeared when you navigated to them. Now each Google event carries its real date and shows up only in its own month — flip to next month and its events are there; flip back and this month's events stay put. Your hand-added family events still repeat every month like before."

- Added / Changed: `src/app/calendar/page.tsx` (day-cell className changed from the either/or ternary `is-selected : is-today` to independent flags so a cell can carry BOTH classes), `src/app/globals.css` (today circle un-scoped from `:not(.is-selected)` — it now always renders on today; NEW `.calendar-day-btn.is-selected .calendar-day-number.is-today-number` adds a 2px white ring so the solid accent circle separates from the accent-tinted tile; NEW `.calendar-day-btn.is-selected.is-today` replaces the dense selected gradient with a clear translucent accent box — 20% fill dark / 22% light, 60%+white accent border, inset white ring, softer accent glow, scale 1.06 kept; NEW matching overrides in both light blocks since `:root[data-theme="light"] .is-today` outranks the base rule; event dots in selected-today switch to accent color so they stay visible on the translucent tint)

Pick any other day and today keeps its circle while the selection keeps its full highlight."

### UI Change Record — 2026-08-26 — Console hygiene: theme script via next/script + Calendar hydration mismatch fixed
- Added / Changed: `src/app/layout.tsx` (anti-FOUC theme-init script moved from raw `<script dangerouslySetInnerHTML>` — which React 19 warns about during client rendering — to `next/script` with `strategy="beforeInteractive"`, the Next.js 16 app-router-supported pattern that injects the inline script into the server HTML head and runs it before hydration), `src/app/calendar/page.tsx` (NEW `mounted` flag via `useSyncExternalStore(subscribeNoop, clientTrue, serverFalse)` — server snapshot false, client snapshot true, no effect/setState so the strict `react-hooks/set-state-in-effect` rule stays clean; NEW `renderEvents`/`renderSchedules` memos that serve the deterministic fallbacks (`events` / `getInitialSchedules()`) until mount and the localStorage-seeded state after; `dayEventMap`, `selectedEvents`, `upcomingByDay`, `sortedSchedules`, and the routines count/empty-state render sites now read the gated values — state, CRUD handlers, and the localStorage write-back effects still use the real state)
- Visual / Motion: No visual change. Fixes two dev-console errors: (1) "Encountered a script tag while rendering React component" at RootLayout, and (2) "Hydration failed because the server rendered HTML didn't match the client" in the month-grid day dots — root cause was `calEvents`/`schedules` lazy-initializing from localStorage on the client (stored events) while the server rendered the fallback data, so dot counts and event rows differed at hydration. Theme behavior unchanged: data-theme is still set pre-hydration (verified at domcontentloaded in dark and light). Stored events/schedules appear one frame after mount instead of at hydration — no flash because server HTML and first client render are identical fallbacks.
- Color sources: None — no color changes.
- Agent action required: Update this section + Change Log. Convention: localStorage-seeded render data must be mounted-gated (this page now uses the useSyncExternalStore variant because the strict set-state-in-effect rule flags the classic `useEffect(() => setMounted(true))` when the flag feeds memo cascades).
- User-facing description (copy-paste ready for responses):
  > "Two console errors are gone. The theme script now uses Next.js's official pre-hydration script slot (same behavior — no theme flash), and the calendar no longer mismatches between server and browser when you have saved events: the grid renders the default data for the first paint and swaps in your saved events immediately after."

### UI Change Record — 2026-08-26 — Motion system: "glass settles" (full-dashboard motion dive)
- Added / Changed: `src/app/globals.css` (NEW ease tokens `--ease-spring: cubic-bezier(0.34,1.56,0.64,1)` + `--ease-settle: cubic-bezier(0.16,1,0.3,1)` in `@theme`; all 16 ad-hoc spring-bezier spellings in globals.css migrated to `var(--ease-spring)`; NEW keyframes `pageSettle` (opacity 0→1 + translateY 10px→0 + blur 6px→0, 240ms settle), `panelSwap` (fade + 6px rise, 200ms), `modalExit` (scale .97 + 10px drop + fade, 150ms), `overlayExit`, `toastExit` (reverse slide, 180ms); NEW utilities `.page-settle` / `.panel-swap`, both added to the `prefers-reduced-motion` kill block), `src/components/ui/PageShell.tsx` (now a client component — `usePathname()` keys `<main>` so every route change remounts and replays the 240ms settle; chat page got the class on its own shell), `src/components/ui/Modal.tsx` (REAL exit animation: internal `closed/open/closing` phase machine — on `open`→false it plays modalExit + overlayExit for 150ms then unmounts; reduced-motion unmounts instantly; enter now `0.35s var(--ease-spring)` via inline style), `src/components/ui/Toast.tsx` (the old close path froze the toast for 200ms then popped it — now plays `toastExit` 180ms inside that window; reduced-motion instant), `src/app/calendar/page.tsx` + `src/app/meals/page.tsx` + `src/app/tasks/page.tsx` + `src/app/ha/page.tsx` (tab panels wrapped in `key={tab}` `.panel-swap` divs — Calendar⇄Schedule, Meals/Grocery/Pantry/Recipes, Tasks⇄Leaderboard, and all five HA tabs now cross-fade with a 6px rise instead of hard-swapping), `src/app/chat/page.tsx` (page-settle on its custom shell), `src/app/page.tsx` (MemberPickerModal now rendered unconditionally with `open={pickerOpen}` so its exit can play — Modal owns its mount lifecycle), `src/components/leaderboard/ShareCard.tsx` (removed `if (!open) return null` guard — it defeated the exit), `src/components/suggestions/SuggestionPinModal.tsx` (guard replaced with a `session` counter that remounts `PinModalInner` per open — keeps the fresh-input semantics AND plays the exit; `open` is now threaded into PinModalInner, which previously resolved a bare `open` identifier to `window.open` (always truthy) — latent bug the old guard masked).
- Visual / Motion: The dashboard now has one coherent motion language — "glass settles": every route arrival fades in with a 10px rise and a 6px blur-out over 240ms (the frosted-glass signature), every tab panel swap is a quick 200ms fade-and-rise, modals exit with a 150ms settle-down (faster than their 350ms spring entrance), and toasts slide back up 180ms when dismissed instead of freezing then vanishing. The capsule nav pill keeps the only spring curve in the app; everything else uses decelerating settles. No new loops, no scroll-driven effects, no page-load choreography — all new motion is one-shot transform/opacity/filter, and every piece is neutralized under `prefers-reduced-motion` (modals/toasts close instantly).
- Color sources: None — motion-only change.
- Agent action required: Update this section + §1.3 + Change Log. Motion convention going forward: use `--ease-spring` for the one playful signature (nav pill) and `--ease-settle` for all arrivals; exits are 150–180ms; don't add new infinite loops outside the weather/chat ambient systems.
- User-facing description (copy-paste ready for responses):
  > "The whole dashboard got a motion pass. Pages now glide in with a soft glass-focus effect when you switch tabs instead of hard-swapping, the panels inside Calendar, Meals, Tasks, and House fade gently when you switch views, pop-up windows settle away smoothly instead of vanishing, and notification banners slide off instead of freezing. Everything respects the reduced-motion setting — with it on, everything is instant."

### UI Change Record — 2026-08-18 — Settings Layout & display: reorder arrows work with real clicks (drag scoped to the ⋮⋮ handle)
- Added / Changed: `src/app/settings/page.tsx` — the Visible-group widget rows were whole-row `draggable` (`draggable` + `onDragStart={handleDragStart(widget.id)}` on the row `<div>`). Any mousedown + micro-movement on a child control (the ↑/↓ arrows, the visibility toggles) started an HTML5 drag on the row, which cancelled the child's click — so the arrows appeared dead in real use (human clicks almost always include 2–5px of movement; only pixel-perfect programmatic clicks worked). Fix: `draggable` + `onDragStart` moved from the row `<div>` onto the ⋮⋮ handle `<span>` (the element with `cursor-grab` / "Drag to reorder" tooltip). `onDragOver` / `onDragLeave` / `onDrop` / `onDragEnd` stay on the row (`dragend` bubbles up from the handle, so the drop-target highlight + drag-end cleanup are unchanged).
- Visual / Motion: No visual change — arrows, toggles, and drag all look identical; only the click behavior is fixed. Verified via Playwright: (1) plain click on ↑/↓ reorders; (2) a jittered click (mousedown + 4px move + mouseup) now also reorders — before the fix the event log showed `mousedown: BUTTON → dragstart: DIV` with no `click` ever firing; (3) drag from the ⋮⋮ handle still reorders (event log: `dragstart:SPAN → dragenter/dragover → drop:SPAN → dragend:SPAN`; Morning Briefing dragged to position 3 correctly).
- Color sources: None — pure interaction change.
- Agent action required: Update this section + "Current Dashboard Snapshot" + Change Log.
- User-facing description (copy-paste ready for responses):
  > "The up/down reorder arrows in Settings → Layout & display now work reliably. Before, the entire widget row was draggable, so a tiny mouse wiggle while clicking an arrow turned the click into a drag and the arrow did nothing. Dragging still works — from the ⋮⋮ handle."

### UI Change Record — 2026-08-18 — Kitchen "orb" true root cause fixed (glass ::before position containment)
- Added / Changed: `src/app/globals.css` — added `position: relative` to the three Apple-material base classes (`.material-thin, .glass-subtle`, `.material-regular, .glass`, `.material-thick, .glass-strong`). Also calmed the specular/glow values while investigating (`.glass::before` white 0.05→0.02, `.liquid-glass::before` 0.06→0.02 in both `globals.css` and the dead `src/styles/materials.css`, `.widget-card` outer glow 40px@22%→20px@12% dark / 48px@28%→24px@14% hover / 32px@16%→16px@8% light).
- Visual / Motion: The persistent top-left "orb" on the Meals and Pantry tabs was NOT the accent glow shadows (removed 2026-08-14) nor the specular opacity. Root cause (found via Playwright DOM inspection): `.glass` elements are `position: static`, but their `::before` specular layer is `position: absolute; inset: 0`. With no positioned ancestor on the card, `inset: 0` resolved against the nearest positioned ancestor — `<main class="relative z-10">` (the full 1440×1810 page) — so every `.glass` card painted a giant `radial-gradient(120% 60% at 20% 0%)` glow across the entire top-left of the page. Meals/Pantry have `.glass` cards near the top (stat tiles, meal/pantry cards); Grocery's top cards are `.widget-card` (no `::before`), which is why Grocery always looked clean. Adding `position: relative` to the material classes contains each `::before` inside its own card, restoring the intended subtle top sheen and removing the page-wide orb. Verified via Playwright screenshots: Meals + Pantry top-left now flat dark, identical to Grocery.
- Color sources: None new — the specular sheen values are unchanged in hue; only containment (position) and a calm-down of opacity/spread.
- Agent action required: Update this section + "Current Dashboard Snapshot" + Change Log.
- User-facing description (copy-paste ready for responses):
  > "The soft glow of light in the top-left of the Kitchen (Meals and Pantry) screens is finally gone. It turned out each frosted glass card was accidentally painting its highlight across the whole page instead of just inside itself — a one-line CSS fix (positioning) now keeps every card's sheen inside the card, so the background stays clean and dark, matching the Grocery tab."

### UI Change Record — 2026-08-14 — Kitchen top-left "orb" glow removed
- Added / Changed: `src/components/meals/MealsTab.tsx` (active "All" pill + active meal-type pills), `src/components/meals/PantryTab.tsx` (active section card), `src/components/meals/RecipesTab.tsx` (active filter chips + "＋ Add to day" button + favorite button) — all dropped their accent glow shadows (`shadow-lg shadow-[var(--color-accent-selected)]/25`, `boxShadow: 0 8px 24px slotColor40`, `shadow-[0_0_20px_var(--color-accent-selected)]/10`, favorite `shadow-lg`). The accent fill, border, and white text stay so the selected state is unchanged — only the circular glow is gone.

Investigation showed the glow shadows behind the active filter pill (Meals), the active section card (Pantry), and the active chips (Recipes) — the brightest accent-glowing elements at the top of each tab — matched the report exactly (Grocery's equivalent glow sits lower on the page, outside the top-left corner, which is why Grocery always looked clean). ### UI Change Record — 2026-08-11 — Three-mode Home layouts: phone / tablet / desktop
- Added / Changed: `src/lib/layout-config.ts` — `LayoutMode` = `"phone" | "tablet" | "desktop"`; new threshold constants `PHONE_MAX_WIDTH = 700` (catches iPad mini 744/768; no phone is ≥700px portrait) and `TABLET_MAX_WIDTH = 1280` (covers iPad Pro 12.9″ = 1024, Nest Hub = 1024); new pure `computeLayoutMode(isPortrait, width)` (unit-tested): portrait <700 → phone, portrait 700–1279 → tablet, landscape OR ≥1280 → desktop; `HomeLayoutConfig` shape → `{ phone: { widgets }, tablet: { widgets }, desktop: { widgets } }`; new `TABLET_COL_SPANS` (morningBriefing / weather / consuelaSuggestions `col-span-2`, everything else 1) + `toTabletOrder` (hole-free full-width-first partition of a single-column order); `homeGridClass` / `widgetSpanClass` / `homeFooterSpanClass` now take a `LayoutMode` — tablet = `"grid grid-cols-2 gap-6 auto-rows-min"` + `col-span-N` (+ footer `col-span-2`), phone = `grid-cols-1` + `""`, desktop = the existing filmstrip classes; `loadLayoutConfig` migration — v1 `{ widgets }` maps into ALL three buckets, v2 `{ portrait, landscape }` maps phone=portrait / desktop=landscape / tablet=partitioned portrait, v3 (app-written) round-trips exactly with id-validation only (no self-heal reorder on the v3 path); plain JSON via `saveLayoutConfig`, no version constant. `src/hooks/useOrientation.tsx` — now returns `{ orientation: LayoutMode; mounted }`, SSR defaults to desktop, listeners on the portrait media query + resize. `src/hooks/useHomeLayout.tsx` — mode-aware `widgetsFor(mode)` lists + mutators; the old `Orientation` type name is fully removed. `src/app/page.tsx` — renders the live mode's grid via the mode-aware helpers; pre-mount `HOME_GRID_FALLBACK` (`grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3`) keeps the SSR frame stable. `src/app/settings/page.tsx` — "Layout & display" gains 📱 Phone / 📱 Tablet / 🖥️ Desktop `SegmentedControl` tabs; reset toast now reads "phone, tablet, and desktop"; export JSON includes all three buckets. `tests/unit/layout-config.test.ts` — rewritten 3-mode contract (23 tests; replaces the 4 stale failures left by the 2026-08-07 filmstrip change).
- Visual / Motion: A tablet held in portrait (700–1279px wide — iPad mini, iPad Pro 12.9″, Nest Hub) no longer shows the tall single-column phone feed: it's now a **2-column pastel bento** — the morning briefing, weather, and Consuela's suggestions each stretch across the full row (col-span-2) and the remaining cards pair up beneath them with no holes; the This Week footer spans both columns. The tablet order is derived from the phone order (full-width widgets keep their relative order first, then the rest pair up), so a user's phone arrangement carries over automatically. Phones (portrait <700px) keep the familiar single-column vertical stack and desktop (landscape, or portrait ≥1280px) keeps the horizontal filmstrip — both unchanged. No new motion; reduced-motion untouched.
- Color sources: None — pure layout change.
- Agent action required: Update this section + "Current Dashboard Snapshot" + Change Log.
- User-facing description (copy-paste ready for responses):
  > "The Home dashboard now remembers three separate card layouts — one for your phone, one for your tablet, and one for your desktop. On a tablet held upright the widgets arrange themselves into a tidy two-column grid: the morning briefing, weather, and Consuela's suggestions stretch across the full row with the rest paired up underneath, and your phone order carries over automatically. Desktop keeps the sideways filmstrip and phones keep scrolling vertically, exactly as before. In Settings → Layout & display you can switch between Phone, Tablet, and Desktop tabs to reorder each layout independently, and Reset restores all three."

### UI Change Record — 2026-08-06 — Widget cards: WCAG contrast fixes + standardized padding
- Added / Changed: `src/app/globals.css` (.widget-card scoped text tokens + `--widget-accent`/`--widget-accent-strong` + `.widget-accent-text` + `.schedule-row` scrim), `src/components/ui/ScheduleDisplay.tsx` (rows get `schedule-row` scrim class, tint moved to inline `backgroundImage`, time text `text-text-secondary`→`text-text-primary`), `src/components/patterns/DayStrip.tsx` (active day uses `--color-accent-button` fill/border/shadow instead of accent-selected), `src/components/ui/Chip.tsx` (accent tone text → `widget-accent-text`; selected fill → `--color-accent-button`), `src/components/suggestions/HomeSuggestionsWidget.tsx` + `HomeLeaderboardWidget.tsx` + `MorningBriefingWidget.tsx` + `src/app/page.tsx` (accent-colored action links/badge/arrow → `widget-accent-text`), `src/components/meals/CurrentMealWidget.tsx` (time badge + tag colors → `var(--widget-accent-strong, accent)`), padding standardization (`SectionCard` footer `p-4`→`p-5`; `ScheduleDisplay` header drops `pb-4`; `CurrentMealWidget` content `p-5 pt-4 pl-14`→`p-5 pl-14`; `StatTile` `px-4 py-3`→`p-4`).
- Visual / Motion: WCAG 2.1 AA (4.5:1) for all text on widget cards in dark AND light mode: muted/secondary text is brightened (dark) / darkened (light) via card-scoped `--color-text-*` overrides; accent-colored text (See all, arrows, chips, briefing badge) uses the accessible `--widget-accent` variant (lightened on dark, darkened on light); DayStrip active day + selected chips use `--color-accent-button` so white text clears 4.5:1; schedule rows gained a solid dark/light scrim under their color tint so the animated Vanta fog can't wash them to a mid-tone. Card padding is now uniform: headers `p-5 pl-14`, bodies `p-5`, footers `p-5`, stat tiles `p-4` — every card title starts at the same x-offset. No new motion; reduced-motion untouched.
- Color sources: `--color-text-secondary: #a5afc7` / `--color-text-muted: #8b99b5` (dark cards), `#57637a` / `#4e596b` (light cards); `--widget-accent` = accent 45% white (dark) / 72% black (light); `--widget-accent-strong` = tone 65% white (dark) / 60% black (light); scrim `rgba(13,17,25,0.72)` dark / `rgba(255,255,255,0.75)` light.
- Agent action required: Update this section + "Current Dashboard Snapshot" + Change Log.

And the temperature number (plus the 5-day forecast highs) is now the same warm orange as the °F badge with a dark glow, sitting on a softly frosted panel, so it reads clearly against the bright summer sky instead of getting lost in white."

### UI Change Record — 2026-08-07 — Landscape filmstrip (scroll left↔right instead of down)
- Added / Changed: `src/lib/layout-config.ts` — new `WIDGET_WIDTHS: Record<WidgetId, string>` map (hero `w-[720px]` for weather + This Week footer, `w-[480px]` suggestions, `w-[420px]` current meal, `w-[360px]` the rest); `homeGridClass("landscape")` changed from `"grid grid-cols-3 gap-6 auto-rows-min"` (vertical-flow 3-col bento) to `"flex gap-6 overflow-x-auto pb-4 pt-6 items-start snap-x snap-proximity"` (horizontal filmstrip); `widgetSpanClass` returns `shrink-0 snap-start ${WIDGET_WIDTHS[id]}` in landscape (was `col-span-N`); `homeFooterSpanClass` returns `shrink-0 snap-start w-[720px]` in landscape (was `col-span-3`). Portrait (`"grid grid-cols-1 gap-6 auto-rows-min"` + `""` spans) unchanged — single-column vertical stack. `HOME_GRID_FALLBACK` + `WIDGET_SPANS` retained for the SSR/pre-mount frame. `page.tsx` unchanged (already consumes `gridClass` / `widgetSpanClass` / `homeFooterSpanClass`).
- Visual / Motion: On a wide screen (landscape orientation) the Home dashboard no longer flows vertically as a 3-column bento you scroll DOWN through. It's now one horizontal filmstrip: each widget is a fixed-width column (360–720px) sitting side-by-side, extending off the right edge of the viewport, so you scroll/swipe LEFT↔RIGHT to reveal the next widget. Gentle `snap-proximity` + `snap-start` lets each card settle to the left edge as you scroll past it. `pt-6` padding keeps each card's protruding top-left icon (which sits ~22px above the card) from being clipped by the horizontal scroll container's implicit `overflow-y: auto`. Portrait (phone) is unchanged — widgets stack vertically and you scroll up/down as before. The This Week card becomes the last filmstrip item (~720px wide) instead of a full-width footer row. No new motion keyframes; reduced-motion untouched (snap is a scroll behavior, not animation).
- Color sources: None — pure layout change.
- Agent action required: Update this section + "Current Dashboard Snapshot" + Change Log.
- User-facing description (copy-paste ready for responses):
  > "On your computer (or anything in landscape), the Home dashboard now scrolls sideways. Instead of stacking the widgets into a tall 3-column grid you scroll down through, they sit side-by-side as a filmstrip and you swipe left and right to move between them — each card snaps gently to the left edge as you pass it. On your phone in portrait nothing's changed; you still scroll up and down."

### UI Change Record — 2026-08-07 — Full-width desktop shell + uniform bento rhythm
- Added / Changed: `src/components/ui/PageShell.tsx` (desktop cap widened `md:max-w-3xl`→`md:max-w-3xl lg:max-w-none` — the landscape bento now fills the whole viewport on desktop, matching the fixed Emergency button's true-screen-edge alignment; mobile `max-w-lg` and the portrait tablet `md:max-w-3xl` band are unchanged), `src/app/page.tsx` (removed the leftover portrait-only top margins on four widgets — `MorningBriefingSlot` had `mt-3 lg:mt-0`, the leaderboard and consuelaSuggestions cases had `mt-3 lg:mt-0`, and the Today's Events `SectionCard` carried `className="mt-4 lg:mt-0"`; in portrait those added 12–16px on top of the grid's `gap-6` 24px row-gap, producing 36/40px gaps between some cards while landscape was already uniform at 24px via the `lg:mt-0` reset; dropping them leaves the CSS-grid `gap-6` as the single source of truth for inter-widget spacing in both orientations).
- Visual / Motion: On a desktop the Home dashboard no longer reads as a narrow phone column centered with big dead margins — the bento's right edge now lands at the same 16px-from-screen-edge as the Always-on-Home Emergency shield (verified via Playwright at 1440px: both edges at 16px, grid width 1408px). And when you switch between portrait (phone) and landscape (desktop/tablet), the vertical rhythm between widget cards is now a uniform 24px everywhere — the previous 36px and 40px gaps that appeared under the leaderboard / suggestions / Today's Events in portrait are gone. No new motion; reduced-motion untouched; portrait and landscape bento logic (`useOrientation` + `homeGridClass`) unchanged.
- Color sources: None — pure layout/spacing changes.

And the gaps between the cards are now a steady 24px whether you're on your phone or your computer; the uneven bigger gaps that used to appear under the leaderboard, suggestions, and Today's Events in portrait are gone."

### UI Change Record — 2026-08-07 — Compact Events / Tasks / This Week widgets
- Added / Changed: `src/components/patterns/StatTile.tsx` (new `compact` prop — padding `p-4`→`p-3`, icon circle `h-9 w-9 text-lg mb-3`→`h-8 w-8 text-base mb-2`, value `text-2xl`→`text-xl`, label `text-xs`→`text-[11px]`, detail `text-[11px]`→`text-[10px]`, and **`lg:aspect-square` dropped** so compact tiles hug their content instead of staying grid-width squares; default stays `compact=false`, so Meals/Tasks/design-system tiles keep the square look), `src/components/patterns/SectionCard.tsx` (new `compact` prop — header `p-5 pl-14`→`p-4 pl-14` + title `text-base`→`text-sm` + description `text-xs`→`text-[11px]`, body `p-5`→`p-4`, footer `p-5`→`p-4`), `src/components/patterns/DayStrip.tsx` (new `compact` prop — buttons `min-w-16 p-3`→`min-w-12 p-2`, label `text-[11px]`→`text-[10px]`, detail `text-lg`→`text-base`, scroll area `pb-2`→`pb-1`), `src/app/page.tsx` (Home top stat-tile row → `compact` on all three tiles; Today's Events + Tasks cards: `compact` + render `slice(0, 3)` rows; when more rows exist a subtle footer appears — `+N more · See all →` in `widget-accent-text` linking to `/calendar` and `/tasks`; empty states unchanged. This Week footer: `compact` SectionCard + `compact` DayStrip).

The This Week strip keeps all 7 day buttons but each is slimmer, so the full-width footer card is noticeably shorter. No new motion; `.tap-sm` + focus rings preserved on the new footer links; reduced-motion untouched.
